===== REPORT_R2.md =====
# Full-upstream PMA → GA: offline end-to-end closeout (2026-10-13)

Status: **offline target-image fake integration completed; live four-arm experiment not authorized or started**. `execution_authorized=false` remains in both drafts. This report records engineering behavior, not PMA task benefit.

## Baseline and bounded changes

The initial HEAD was `ccb841ea1bb738246493dff8802e70eb45fa7bb3` on `crs-v0-20261007`, matching the requested baseline. The only pre-existing untracked items were `GenericAgent-main/monitor_agent_core/**/__pycache__`; they were retained. Incremental code commits: `74b28dbdca66b9394e9acc85891278e429221131` (public response mapping), `c8402206ba4a9f888b8f755dc4377a6918d9915d` (save and host stop), `87d9b77e9eb219e660ff8b5448e4a4999400ae8b` (unarmed Harbor launcher and reference comparison). The final audit commit is reported separately in the handoff.

The fixed PMA source is the prior unchanged 213-file vendor tree at `89e5c0d6aadfe531a1aee42fd290d48be89973dd` (Apache-2.0). No upstream method prompt, bank, trigger, renderer, or two-phase algorithm changed. The production changes are confined to the already default-off `GenericAgent-main/agentmain.py` bridge termination ordering and a terminal receipt field; the Task implementation, benchmark instructions, official solution and verifier were not edited. Existing `agent_loop.py` still maps one GA actor attempt, including all tools, to one PMA step.

## Concrete fixes and limits

`PMAEpisode._actor_step` maps the entire public GA `response.content` into upstream `analysis` only when GA has no structured analysis; `plan` remains absent. The original event is retained verbatim. Hidden `response.thinking` is never read. An independent original `_run_agent_loop` fixture and actual GA event adapter produced equal phase-1/phase-2 prompts, system texts and serialized tool schema for a distinctive public response, two tools, two results, and a parse-error actor-attempt hole. Separate original-loop comparisons cover initial phase-1 and phase-2 injected failures; lower transport audit records each phase status while leaving upstream's catch-and-continue behavior intact. This does not prove every original Terminus2 feature maps to GA (e.g. its actor-only summarization remains unused).

GA now calls `task_end` before publishing `done`/`[ROUND END]`. It forwards the original terminal `result` to the JSON event instead of serializing potentially non-JSON tool data; the full original Task outcome remains in GA's local completion path. The Task terminal receipt now has a separate `pma_finalization` field. A successful PMA end is not inferred from `CURRENT_TASK_DONE`: the host worker must actually save both `memory.json` and `trajectory_memory.json`, write the event receipt, then exit. A second-artifact save failure records `save_failed` and never marks a clean PMA end. Worker exit and saved state are distinct from Task completion and from Harbor return code.

The worker has its own absolute deadline, stop file, in-flight cancellation attempt and host-only fallback save. A deadline in-flight test produced no late Task receipt, marked remote execution unknown and saved local state without claiming a normal `task_end`. If an underlying provider ignores cancellation, the launcher has a ten-second grace then kills that worker; absence of a worker exit receipt is a failure, not clean shutdown. No later PMA result is sent after the deadline. The fallback save performs no model call. Original LiteLLM retry and upstream phase failure handling are unchanged; a remote call's service-side completion or cost can remain unknown.

`offline_launcher.py` is unarmed and rejects `--execution-authorized`. It selects the SHA-pinned research Harbor adapter, creates a fresh public task package and isolated source bundle, starts one host worker only for B, mounts only request RW/response RO into the Task container, routes GA inference to the existing local Unix-socket fake gateway, then verifies worker exit and absence of owned Docker containers. It does not create a Monitor, invoke a native verifier, or install the vendor PMA into GA. The worker's private bank, source tree, raw prompts and credential configuration are outside all recorded Task mounts. Actual Docker inspect shows the main container on each target image, network `none`, request RW, response RO, no host-private mount. The official image `/tests` and `/solution` are absent before Harbor setup; the public Harbor package has empty test/solution directories. Task-side write access to its own request spool is a protocol trust limit, not an OS proof against malicious event forgery.

## Image and task identity

Both exact registered official tags now pulled successfully (the former registry timeout was connectivity, not proven authorization denial):

- DB: `alexgshaw/db-wal-recovery:20251031`, image ID and RepoDigest `sha256:0e33ea5ec823975d1bd6c3778395c9f94251dd88f571146057bff6adb7e4594e`.
- Code: `alexgshaw/fix-code-vulnerability:20251031`, image ID and RepoDigest `sha256:cac325252991f823713b2d0441502972901dd782bd67f66c03d9b1e410dac5c0`.

The official task source remains fixed at `2fd12b88aafdd04a52c298e3940bcb189f9766d6`. Original `task.toml` declares 900-second agent and verifier limits per task. The offline Harbor test used a common 180-second fake-agent ceiling and two GA turns solely for wiring; these are not proposed real experiment truncations. The future four-arm draft proposes a common 3600-second agent wall-clock ceiling and 300 Task turns, subject to separate approval. PMA waiting consumes that same wall clock, so equal ceilings do not mean equal active Task time. Actual real endpoint Opus 4.6 compatibility and its billing/latency are unverified.

## Offline calls and actual evidence

Selected final integration roots outside Git: `E:/pma-ga-offline-db-a-20261013-r2`, `E:/pma-ga-offline-db-b-20261013-r6`, `E:/pma-ga-offline-code-b-20261013-r4`, `E:/pma-ga-offline-code-a-20261013`. Their sanitized requests/SSE, worker event and transport receipts, terminal record, actual mounts and stdout/stderr are copied into `offline_evidence_r2/` with per-file SHA-256 in `FILE_HASHES_R2.json`. The two B roots each have `task_start → before_actor_call → actor_step(completed) → task_end(saved)` and two fake PMA memory calls. The captured full first Task request in each B root contains the fake reminder. The A roots have no worker. Actual main-container image ID matches the registered target image in each case. A/B same-task first request system, tools, model and generation fields agree; B's initial public Task text begins with exactly A's input and then includes the original PMA reminder (here produced by a fake memory transport). The full structures, not only hashes, are preserved.

Failed development fixtures were not overwritten: `E:/pma-ga-offline-db-b-20261013` failed before Harbor due Windows GBK rendering of Rich output; `...-r2` and `...-r3` reached Harbor but PMA `task_end` failed with a `TypeError` from a non-JSON terminal data field. The latter displayed Task `CURRENT_TASK_DONE` but worker stopped via host fallback and **did not** receive `task_end`. `...-r4` first verified the repaired save-before-done ordering; `...-r6` rechecked both actual mounts and cleanup. These failures are engineering fixture attempts, not extra real PMA/Task trials; the local originals remain for audit. The pre-fix rich traceback is not copied wholesale because it may include private process locals; its error type and run path are recorded here.

`TEST_RESULTS_R2.txt` holds commands, exit codes and complete captured stdout/stderr for the eleven upstream/adapter unittests and twenty existing GA-loop tests. One interim reference fixture run failed due a missing fake `chat` method; after fixing only that fixture, the reference comparison passed. The actual original-loop comparison covers initial trigger, one ordinary multi-tool step, a parse-error hole, the final normal budget step in an earlier test, and phase failure prompts. It does **not** compare every source logger field/trajectory byte or prove true endpoint behavior; time and random IDs are not used as semantic evidence. Source reuse correctness and protocol matching are separate from real PMA effectiveness.

Real Task Agent, Supervisor, PMA/provider API, native evaluator, training and scoring-model calls in this stage: **0**. Offline Harbor launched GA with deterministic local fake Task responses in four fresh task containers. Fake Task provider captures: one completed request per selected root; fake PMA memory calls: two per B root. No official solution or verifier ran. The host-private source profile was used only to construct an isolated local fake Task profile; its credential bytes and endpoint are absent from this audit and from the Task container.

## Remaining live-start questions

This stage does not authorize or certify a live four-arm run. The unarmed launcher cannot be used to bypass approval. A separately audited live launch must bind four fresh run IDs, manifest and code hashes, real PMA 4.6 endpoint/profile behavior, a shared wall-clock budget, and a verified native-evaluation barrier to each final frozen artifact. Official verifier outputs are not present and cannot be inferred from Harbor `return_code=0`. A task-side process sharing the GA container can see the spool request mount; this design assumes Task code does not adversarially forge PMA protocol events. If adversarial authenticity is required, that trust boundary needs an independent design decision before live use. Old Fyne/Kitex scores and this scripted fixture are not controls for the future Terminal-Bench comparison.

+# Full-upstream PMA → GA: offline end-to-end closeout (2026-10-13)
+
+Status: **offline target-image fake integration completed; live four-arm experiment not authorized or started**. `execution_authorized=false` remains in both drafts. This report records engineering behavior, not PMA task benefit.
+
+## Baseline and bounded changes
+
+The initial HEAD was `ccb841ea1bb738246493dff8802e70eb45fa7bb3` on `crs-v0-20261007`, matching the requested baseline. The only pre-existing untracked items were `GenericAgent-main/monitor_agent_core/**/__pycache__`; they were retained. Incremental code commits: `74b28dbdca66b9394e9acc85891278e429221131` (public response mapping), `c8402206ba4a9f888b8f755dc4377a6918d9915d` (save and host stop), `87d9b77e9eb219e660ff8b5448e4a4999400ae8b` (unarmed Harbor launcher and reference comparison). The final audit commit is reported separately in the handoff.
+
+The fixed PMA source is the prior unchanged 213-file vendor tree at `89e5c0d6aadfe531a1aee42fd290d48be89973dd` (Apache-2.0). No upstream method prompt, bank, trigger, renderer, or two-phase algorithm changed. The production changes are confined to the already default-off `GenericAgent-main/agentmain.py` bridge termination ordering and a terminal receipt field; the Task implementation, benchmark instructions, official solution and verifier were not edited. Existing `agent_loop.py` still maps one GA actor attempt, including all tools, to one PMA step.
+
+## Concrete fixes and limits
+
+`PMAEpisode._actor_step` maps the entire public GA `response.content` into upstream `analysis` only when GA has no structured analysis; `plan` remains absent. The original event is retained verbatim. Hidden `response.thinking` is never read. An independent original `_run_agent_loop` fixture and actual GA event adapter produced equal phase-1/phase-2 prompts, system texts and serialized tool schema for a distinctive public response, two tools, two results, and a parse-error actor-attempt hole. Separate original-loop comparisons cover initial phase-1 and phase-2 injected failures; lower transport audit records each phase status while leaving upstream's catch-and-continue behavior intact. This does not prove every original Terminus2 feature maps to GA (e.g. its actor-only summarization remains unused).
+
+GA now calls `task_end` before publishing `done`/`[ROUND END]`. It forwards the original terminal `result` to the JSON event instead of serializing potentially non-JSON tool data; the full original Task outcome remains in GA's local completion path. The Task terminal receipt now has a separate `pma_finalization` field. A successful PMA end is not inferred from `CURRENT_TASK_DONE`: the host worker must actually save both `memory.json` and `trajectory_memory.json`, write the event receipt, then exit. A second-artifact save failure records `save_failed` and never marks a clean PMA end. Worker exit and saved state are distinct from Task completion and from Harbor return code.
+
+The worker has its own absolute deadline, stop file, in-flight cancellation attempt and host-only fallback save. A deadline in-flight test produced no late Task receipt, marked remote execution unknown and saved local state without claiming a normal `task_end`. If an underlying provider ignores cancellation, the launcher has a ten-second grace then kills that worker; absence of a worker exit receipt is a failure, not clean shutdown. No later PMA result is sent after the deadline. The fallback save performs no model call. Original LiteLLM retry and upstream phase failure handling are unchanged; a remote call's service-side completion or cost can remain unknown.
+
+`offline_launcher.py` is unarmed and rejects `--execution-authorized`. It selects the SHA-pinned research Harbor adapter, creates a fresh public task package and isolated source bundle, starts one host worker only for B, mounts only request RW/response RO into the Task container, routes GA inference to the existing local Unix-socket fake gateway, then verifies worker exit and absence of owned Docker containers. It does not create a Monitor, invoke a native verifier, or install the vendor PMA into GA. The worker's private bank, source tree, raw prompts and credential configuration are outside all recorded Task mounts. Actual Docker inspect shows the main container on each target image, network `none`, request RW, response RO, no host-private mount. The official image `/tests` and `/solution` are absent before Harbor setup; the public Harbor package has empty test/solution directories. Task-side write access to its own request spool is a protocol trust limit, not an OS proof against malicious event forgery.
+
+## Image and task identity
+
+Both exact registered official tags now pulled successfully (the former registry timeout was connectivity, not proven authorization denial):
+
+- DB: `alexgshaw/db-wal-recovery:20251031`, image ID and RepoDigest `sha256:0e33ea5ec823975d1bd6c3778395c9f94251dd88f571146057bff6adb7e4594e`.
+- Code: `alexgshaw/fix-code-vulnerability:20251031`, image ID and RepoDigest `sha256:cac325252991f823713b2d0441502972901dd782bd67f66c03d9b1e410dac5c0`.
+
+The official task source remains fixed at `2fd12b88aafdd04a52c298e3940bcb189f9766d6`. Original `task.toml` declares 900-second agent and verifier limits per task. The offline Harbor test used a common 180-second fake-agent ceiling and two GA turns solely for wiring; these are not proposed real experiment truncations. The future four-arm draft proposes a common 3600-second agent wall-clock ceiling and 300 Task turns, subject to separate approval. PMA waiting consumes that same wall clock, so equal ceilings do not mean equal active Task time. Actual real endpoint Opus 4.6 compatibility and its billing/latency are unverified.
+
+## Offline calls and actual evidence
+
+Selected final integration roots outside Git: `E:/pma-ga-offline-db-a-20261013-r2`, `E:/pma-ga-offline-db-b-20261013-r6`, `E:/pma-ga-offline-code-b-20261013-r4`, `E:/pma-ga-offline-code-a-20261013`. Their sanitized requests/SSE, worker event and transport receipts, terminal record, actual mounts and stdout/stderr are copied into `offline_evidence_r2/` with per-file SHA-256 in `FILE_HASHES_R2.json`. The two B roots each have `task_start → before_actor_call → actor_step(completed) → task_end(saved)` and two fake PMA memory calls. The captured full first Task request in each B root contains the fake reminder. The A roots have no worker. Actual main-container image ID matches the registered target image in each case. A/B same-task first request system, tools, model and generation fields agree; B's initial public Task text begins with exactly A's input and then includes the original PMA reminder (here produced by a fake memory transport). The full structures, not only hashes, are preserved.
+
+Failed development fixtures were not overwritten: `E:/pma-ga-offline-db-b-20261013` failed before Harbor due Windows GBK rendering of Rich output; `...-r2` and `...-r3` reached Harbor but PMA `task_end` failed with a `TypeError` from a non-JSON terminal data field. The latter displayed Task `CURRENT_TASK_DONE` but worker stopped via host fallback and **did not** receive `task_end`. `...-r4` first verified the repaired save-before-done ordering; `...-r6` rechecked both actual mounts and cleanup. These failures are engineering fixture attempts, not extra real PMA/Task trials; the local originals remain for audit. The pre-fix rich traceback is not copied wholesale because it may include private process locals; its error type and run path are recorded here.
+
+`TEST_RESULTS_R2.txt` holds commands, exit codes and complete captured stdout/stderr for the eleven upstream/adapter unittests and twenty existing GA-loop tests. One interim reference fixture run failed due a missing fake `chat` method; after fixing only that fixture, the reference comparison passed. The actual original-loop comparison covers initial trigger, one ordinary multi-tool step, a parse-error hole, the final normal budget step in an earlier test, and phase failure prompts. It does **not** compare every source logger field/trajectory byte or prove true endpoint behavior; time and random IDs are not used as semantic evidence. Source reuse correctness and protocol matching are separate from real PMA effectiveness.
+
+Real Task Agent, Supervisor, PMA/provider API, native evaluator, training and scoring-model calls in this stage: **0**. Offline Harbor launched GA with deterministic local fake Task responses in four fresh task containers. Fake Task provider captures: one completed request per selected root; fake PMA memory calls: two per B root. No official solution or verifier ran. The host-private source profile was used only to construct an isolated local fake Task profile; its credential bytes and endpoint are absent from this audit and from the Task container.
+
+## Remaining live-start questions
+
+This stage does not authorize or certify a live four-arm run. The unarmed launcher cannot be used to bypass approval. A separately audited live launch must bind four fresh run IDs, manifest and code hashes, real PMA 4.6 endpoint/profile behavior, a shared wall-clock budget, and a verified native-evaluation barrier to each final frozen artifact. Official verifier outputs are not present and cannot be inferred from Harbor `return_code=0`. A task-side process sharing the GA container can see the spool request mount; this design assumes Task code does not adversarially forge PMA protocol events. If adversarial authenticity is required, that trust boundary needs an independent design decision before live use. Old Fyne/Kitex scores and this scripted fixture are not controls for the future Terminal-Bench comparison.

+"""Mechanically export only offline, secret-free PMA/GA fixture evidence."""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import hashlib
+import json
+import os
+from pathlib import Path
+import re
+import subprocess
+
+
+STAGE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+REPO = STAGE.parents[2]
+EVIDENCE = STAGE / "offline_evidence_r2"
+RUNS = {
+    "db_A": Path("E:/pma-ga-offline-db-a-20261013-r2"),
+    "db_B": Path("E:/pma-ga-offline-db-b-20261013-r6"),
+    "code_B": Path("E:/pma-ga-offline-code-b-20261013-r4"),
+    "code_A": Path("E:/pma-ga-offline-code-a-20261013"),
+}
+
+
+def sanitized(raw: bytes) -> bytes:
+    text = raw.decode("utf-8", errors="replace")
+    text = re.sub(r"(?i)(authorization|x-api-key)([\"']?\s*[:=]\s*[\"']?)[^\s\"']+",
+                  r"\1\2[REDACTED]", text)
+    text = re.sub(r"\bsk-[A-Za-z0-9_-]{12,}\b", "[REDACTED_KEY]", text)
+    text = re.sub(r"https?://[^\s\"']+", "[REDACTED_URL]", text)
+    return text.encode("utf-8")
+
+
+def save_text(name: str, data: bytes) -> None:
+    target = STAGE / name
+    target.write_bytes(sanitized(data))
+
+
+def run_tests() -> None:
+    commands = [
+        (["C:/Users/hsy/AppData/Local/Temp/pma-ga-py312/Scripts/python.exe",
+          "-m", "unittest", "-v", "test_episode"], STAGE),
+        (["py", "-3.13", "-m", "pytest", "-q",
+          "tests/test_pma_upstream_ga_bridge.py",
+          "tests/test_research_agent_loop.py"], REPO / "GenericAgent-main"),
+    ]
+    sections = []
+    for command, cwd in commands:
+        env = dict(os.environ)
+        env["PYTHONUTF8"] = "1"
+        env["LITELLM_LOCAL_MODEL_COST_MAP"] = "True"
+        result = subprocess.run(command, cwd=cwd, env=env, capture_output=True,
+                                timeout=120)
+        sections.append("COMMAND " + json.dumps(command) + "\nCWD " + str(cwd) +
+                        "\nEXIT " + str(result.returncode) + "\nSTDOUT\n" +
+                        result.stdout.decode("utf-8", "replace") + "\nSTDERR\n" +
+                        result.stderr.decode("utf-8", "replace"))
+    save_text("TEST_RESULTS_R2.txt", "\n\n".join(sections).encode())
+
+
+def export_runs() -> dict:
+    EVIDENCE.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
+    summary = {}
+    for label, root in RUNS.items():
+        target = EVIDENCE / label
+        target.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
+        paths = [root / "archive" / name for name in (
+            "offline_result.json", "actual_container_mounts.json",
+            "harbor.stdout.log", "harbor.stderr.log", "worker.stdout.log",
+            "worker.stderr.log")]
+        paths += sorted((root / "archive" / "fake_provider_requests").glob("*"))
+        if (root / "host_private" / "archive").is_dir():
+            paths += [root / "host_private" / "archive" / name for name in (
+                "accepted.jsonl", "events.jsonl", "memory_transport.jsonl",
+                "memory_payload.jsonl",
+                "memory.json", "trajectory_memory.json")]
+            paths += [root / "host_private" / "worker_exit.json"]
+        paths += list((root / "jobs").rglob("task_terminal.json"))
+        for source in paths:
+            if source.is_file():
+                relative = ("fake_provider_requests/" + source.name
+                            if source.parent.name == "fake_provider_requests" else source.name)
+                destination = target / relative
+                destination.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+                destination.write_bytes(sanitized(source.read_bytes()))
+        result = json.loads((target / "offline_result.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
+        summary[label] = {key: result.get(key) for key in (
+            "image_id", "repo_digests", "harbor_return_code", "worker_return_code",
+            "actual_container_count", "worker_private_mounted", "worker_exit_receipt")}
+        request = json.loads((target / "fake_provider_requests" / "0001.json").read_text(
+            encoding="utf-8"))
+        summary[label]["request_sha256"] = hashlib.sha256(json.dumps(
+            request["body"], ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True,
+            separators=(",", ":")).encode()).hexdigest()
+        summary[label]["provider_capture_count"] = len(list((target /
+            "fake_provider_requests").glob("*.json")))
+    for task in ("db", "code"):
+        a = json.loads((EVIDENCE / f"{task}_A" / "fake_provider_requests" /
+                        "0001.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))["body"]
+        b = json.loads((EVIDENCE / f"{task}_B" / "fake_provider_requests" /
+                        "0001.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))["body"]
+        summary[task + "_parity"] = {
+            "system_equal": a.get("system") == b.get("system"),
+            "tools_equal": a.get("tools") == b.get("tools"),
+            "model_equal": a.get("model") == b.get("model"),
+            "generation_equal": {key: a.get(key) for key in
+                ("max_tokens", "thinking", "temperature", "stream")} ==
+                {key: b.get(key) for key in
+                ("max_tokens", "thinking", "temperature", "stream")},
+            "public_input_prefix_equal": b["messages"][0]["content"][0]["text"].startswith(
+                a["messages"][0]["content"][0]["text"]),
+            "b_reminder_visible_in_first_request": "Offline PMA reminder." in json.dumps(b),
+        }
+    (EVIDENCE / "SUMMARY.json").write_text(json.dumps(summary, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
+    return summary
+
+
+def finish_packet() -> None:
+    files = [path for path in sorted(EVIDENCE.rglob("*")) if path.is_file()]
+    files += [STAGE / name for name in (
+        "episode.py", "event_spool.py", "worker_main.py", "offline_launcher.py",
+        "audit_export_r2.py", "test_episode.py", "harbor_adapter.py",
+        "FOUR_RUN_MANIFEST_PREPARED.json", "REPORT_R2.md", "TEST_RESULTS_R2.txt")]
+    files += [REPO / "GenericAgent-main" / name for name in (
+        "agentmain.py", "agent_loop.py", "pma_upstream_bridge.py")]
+    hashes = {}
+    for path in files:
+        relative = path.relative_to(REPO).as_posix()
+        raw = path.read_bytes()
+        entry = {"sha256": hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest(), "bytes": len(raw)}
+        if path.suffix == ".py" or path.name in (
+                "REPORT_R2.md", "FOUR_RUN_MANIFEST_PREPARED.json"):
+            blob = subprocess.run(["git", "show", "HEAD:" + relative], cwd=REPO,
+                                  capture_output=True, check=True).stdout
+            entry["git_blob_sha256"] = hashlib.sha256(blob).hexdigest()
+            entry["git_blob_bytes"] = len(blob)
+        hashes[relative] = entry
+    (STAGE / "FILE_HASHES_R2.json").write_text(json.dumps(hashes, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
+    diff = subprocess.run(["git", "diff", "ccb841ea1bb738246493dff8802e70eb45fa7bb3",
+                           "HEAD", "--", "GenericAgent-main/agentmain.py",
+                           "method_discovery/curator_supervisor_convergence_v0/"
+                           "pma_full_upstream_ga_v1_20261013"], cwd=REPO,
+                          capture_output=True, check=True).stdout
+    sections = []
+    for name in ("REPORT_R2.md", "FOUR_RUN_MANIFEST_PREPARED.json",
+                 "TEST_RESULTS_R2.txt", "FILE_HASHES_R2.json"):
+        sections.append("===== " + name + " =====\n" +
+                        (STAGE / name).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
+    sections.append("===== FULL DIFF FROM ccb841ea =====\n" + diff.decode("utf-8", "replace"))
+    for path in files:
+        sections.append("===== " + path.relative_to(REPO).as_posix() + " =====\n" +
+                        path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace"))
+    (STAGE / "AUDIT_PACKET_R2.txt").write_text("\n\n".join(sections), encoding="utf-8")
+
+
+def main() -> None:
+    run_tests()
+    summary = export_runs()
+    if not all(summary[f"{task}_parity"][key] for task in ("db", "code") for key in (
+            "system_equal", "tools_equal", "model_equal", "generation_equal",
+            "public_input_prefix_equal", "b_reminder_visible_in_first_request")):
+        raise RuntimeError("A/B offline first request parity failed")
+    finish_packet()
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    main()

+    def _save_final_state(self, terminal: Any, event: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
+        """No-inference finalization; partial bank files never certify success."""
+        self.finalization_attempted = True
+        try:
+            self.agent.save_memory(str(self.archive / "memory.json"))
+            if not all((self.archive / name).is_file() for name in
+                       ("memory.json", "trajectory_memory.json")):
+                raise RuntimeError("upstream did not save both memory artifacts")
+            for name in ("memory.json", "trajectory_memory.json"):
+                with (self.archive / name).open("r", encoding="utf-8") as stream:
+                    if not isinstance(json.load(stream), dict):
+                        raise RuntimeError("upstream memory artifact is not an object")
+        except Exception as exc:
+            self._record(event, {"status": "save_failed", "error_type": type(exc).__name__,
+                                 "terminal": terminal})
+            raise
+        return {"status": "saved", "terminal": terminal,
                 "actor_attempts": self.last_attempt}
+
+    def finalize_without_event(self, reason: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
+        """Bounded host shutdown after ordinary Task event channel is unavailable."""
+        if self.finalization_attempted or not self.started:
+            return {"status": "not_saved", "reason": reason}
+        result = self._save_final_state({"result": "TASK_TERMINAL_UNKNOWN"},
+                                        {"kind": "host_finalize", "reason": reason})
+        self.ended = True
+        return {**result, "status": "saved_without_task_end", "reason": reason}

+"""Unarmed PMA/GA Harbor fixture.  There is deliberately no live mode here."""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import argparse
+import hashlib
+import json
+import os
+from pathlib import Path
+import shutil
+import subprocess
+import time
+
+from long_context_bench.scripts.isolated_run_bundle import build_bundle, digest_tree
+from method_discovery.curator_supervisor_convergence_v0.real_task_effect_pilot_preflight_20261010.pilot_offline_harbor import _offline_gateway
+
+
+TASKS = {
+    "db-wal-recovery": "alexgshaw/db-wal-recovery:20251031",
+    "fix-code-vulnerability": "alexgshaw/fix-code-vulnerability:20251031",
+}
+STAGE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+
+
+def image_identity(tag: str) -> dict:
+    result = subprocess.run(["docker", "image", "inspect", tag], capture_output=True,
+                            text=True, timeout=30, check=True)
+    row = json.loads(result.stdout)[0]
+    return {"image_id": row["Id"], "repo_digests": row.get("RepoDigests") or []}
+
+
+def capture_mounts(root: Path, *, include_stopped: bool = False) -> list[dict]:
+    """Read actual Compose containers, never infer isolation from path names."""
+    found = []
+    listed = subprocess.run(["docker", "ps", "-aq" if include_stopped else "-q"], capture_output=True,
+                            text=True, timeout=10, check=True)
+    for container in listed.stdout.splitlines():
+        inspected = subprocess.run(["docker", "inspect", container], capture_output=True,
+                                   text=True, timeout=10, check=True)
+        row = json.loads(inspected.stdout)[0]
+        config_files = row.get("Config", {}).get("Labels", {}).get(
+            "com.docker.compose.project.config_files", "")
+        if str(root / "bundle" / "isolation.compose.json") not in config_files:
+            continue
+        found.append({"id": row["Id"], "name": row["Name"],
+                      "image_id": row["Image"],
+                      "network_mode": row.get("HostConfig", {}).get("NetworkMode"),
+                      "mounts": [{"source": m.get("Source"), "destination": m.get("Destination"),
+                                  "rw": m.get("RW"), "type": m.get("Type")}
+                                 for m in row.get("Mounts", [])]})
+    return found
+
+
+def clean_owned_containers(root: Path) -> list[str]:
+    """Remove only Compose resources whose config path is this fresh run."""
+    errors = []
+    for row in capture_mounts(root, include_stopped=True):
+        removed = subprocess.run(["docker", "rm", "-f", row["id"]],
+                                 capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=30)
+        if removed.returncode:
+            errors.append(f"remove_failed:{row['id']}")
+            continue
+        checked = subprocess.run(["docker", "inspect", row["id"]],
+                                 capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=30)
+        if checked.returncode == 0 or "no such object" not in checked.stderr.lower():
+            errors.append(f"removal_unconfirmed:{row['id']}")
+    return errors
+
+
+def prepare(args) -> dict:
+    if args.task not in TASKS or args.condition not in {"A", "B"}:
+        raise ValueError("unsupported PMA fixture arm")
+    if args.execution_authorized:
+        raise ValueError("offline launcher cannot authorize model execution")
+    root = Path(args.run_root).resolve(strict=False)
+    root.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=False)
+    (root / "control").mkdir()
+    (root / "archive").mkdir()
+    task = root / "public_task"
+    shutil.copytree(STAGE / "assets" / "public" / args.task, task)
+    (task / "tests").mkdir(exist_ok=True)
+    (task / "solution").mkdir(exist_ok=True)
+    # Task only receives the public task package. The fixed PMA source and
+    # private memory archive never appear in a Compose or Harbor mount.
+    ga_source = Path(args.ga_source).resolve(strict=True)
+    ga_stage = root / "control" / "ga_stage"
+    shutil.copytree(ga_source, ga_stage, ignore=shutil.ignore_patterns(
+        ".git", "temp", "tests", "__pycache__", ".pytest_cache", "*.pyc",
+        "*.local.json", "file_access_stats.json", "mykey.py"))
+    shutil.copy2(Path(args.task_profile_file).resolve(strict=True), ga_stage / "mykey.py")
+    runtime = Path(args.runtime_root).resolve(strict=True)
+    source, compose = build_bundle(root / "bundle", ga_stage, runtime,
+                                   args.python_home, args.task_profile, None, 15340)
+    _offline_gateway(root, runtime, args.python_home)
+    manifest = json.loads((STAGE / "FOUR_RUN_MANIFEST_DRAFT.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
+    if manifest["execution_authorized"] is not False:
+        raise RuntimeError("PMA draft unexpectedly authorized")
+    identity = image_identity(TASKS[args.task])
+    for name in ("requests", "responses"):
+        (root / "control" / "spool" / name).mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=False)
+    config = json.loads(compose.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
+    if args.condition == "B":
+        main = config["services"]["main"]
+        main["volumes"] += [
+            {"type": "bind", "source": (root / "control" / "spool" / "requests").as_posix(),
+             "target": "/run/pma-bridge/requests", "read_only": False},
+            {"type": "bind", "source": (root / "control" / "spool" / "responses").as_posix(),
+             "target": "/run/pma-bridge/responses", "read_only": True},
+        ]
+    compose.write_text(json.dumps(config, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
+    record = {"schema": "pma-ga-offline-harbor-v1", "execution_authorized": False,
+              "run_id": args.run_id, "task": args.task, "condition": args.condition,
+              "image": TASKS[args.task], **identity,
+              "public_task_tree_sha256": digest_tree(task),
+              "ga_bundle_tree_sha256": digest_tree(source),
+              "source_profile_file_sha256": hashlib.sha256(
+                  Path(args.task_profile_file).read_bytes()).hexdigest(),
+              "worker_private_mounted": "not_yet_inspected", "network_mode": "none_requested",
+              "native_evaluator_calls": 0, "fake_provider_only": True}
+    (root / "archive" / "prepared.json").write_text(
+        json.dumps(record, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
+    return record
+

+def run_offline(args) -> dict:
+    record = prepare(args)
+    root = Path(args.run_root).resolve(strict=True)
+    deadline = time.time() + args.timeout_sec
+    worker = None
+    worker_stdout = worker_stderr = None
+    result = None
+    try:
+        if args.condition == "B":
+            worker_stdout = (root / "archive" / "worker.stdout.log").open("wb")
+            worker_stderr = (root / "archive" / "worker.stderr.log").open("wb")
+            env = dict(os.environ)
+            env["LITELLM_LOCAL_MODEL_COST_MAP"] = "True"
+            env["PYTHONPATH"] = os.pathsep.join((str(STAGE),
+                str(STAGE / "vendor" / "pma_89e5c0d" / "src"),
+                str(STAGE / "vendor" / "pma_89e5c0d" / "external" / "harbor")))
+            command = [str(Path(args.worker_python).resolve(strict=True)),
+                       str(STAGE / "worker_main.py"), "--run-id", args.run_id,
+                       "--task-id", args.task, "--private-root", str(root / "host_private"),
+                       "--requests", str(root / "control" / "spool" / "requests"),
+                       "--responses", str(root / "control" / "spool" / "responses"),
+                       "--deadline-epoch", str(deadline), "--fake-memory"]
+            worker = subprocess.Popen(command, cwd=STAGE, env=env,
+                                      stdout=worker_stdout, stderr=worker_stderr)
+            ready = root / "host_private" / "worker_ready.json"
+            ready_deadline = min(deadline, time.time() + 10)
+            while not ready.is_file():
+                if worker.poll() is not None or time.time() >= ready_deadline:
+                    raise RuntimeError("offline PMA worker did not become ready")
+                time.sleep(.05)
+            worker_record = json.loads(ready.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
+            if (worker_record["run_id"], worker_record["task_id"]) != (args.run_id, args.task):
+                raise RuntimeError("offline PMA worker identity mismatch")
+        mounts = [
+            {"type": "bind", "source": str(Path(args.runtime_root).resolve(strict=True)),
+             "target": "/opt/m4-runtime", "read_only": True},
+            {"type": "bind", "source": str((root / "bundle" / "source").resolve(strict=True)),
+             "target": "/opt/genericagent-source", "read_only": True},
+        ]
+        command = [str(Path(args.harbor_exe).resolve(strict=True)), "jobs", "start",
+                   "--job-name", args.run_id, "--jobs-dir", str(root / "jobs"),
+                   "--path", str(root / "public_task"),
+                   "--agent", ("method_discovery.curator_supervisor_convergence_v0."
+                               "pma_full_upstream_ga_v1_20261013.harbor_adapter:HarborGenericAgent"),
+                   "--model", "claude-opus-4-8", "--mounts", json.dumps(mounts),
+                   "--extra-docker-compose", str(root / "bundle" / "isolation.compose.json"),
+                   "--n-concurrent", "1", "--max-retries", "0", "--yes",
+                   "--disable-verification", "--agent-timeout-multiplier",
+                   format(args.timeout_sec / 900, ".15g")]
+        kwargs = {"llm_no": 0, "run_id": args.run_id, "task_id": args.task,
+                  "expected_model": "claude-opus-4-8", "python_home": args.python_home,
+                  "ga_source_sha256": record["ga_bundle_tree_sha256"],
+                  "timeout_sec": args.timeout_sec, "max_turns": 2,
+                  "llm_config_name": args.task_profile, "condition_id": "pma-neutral"}
+        for key, value in kwargs.items():
+            command += ["--ak", f"{key}={value}"]
+        env = dict(os.environ)
+        repo = STAGE.parents[2]
+        env["PYTHONPATH"] = os.pathsep.join((str(repo), str(repo / "GenericAgent-main"),
+                                            str(Path(args.bench_root).resolve(strict=True))))
+        env["GA_RUN_ISOLATION"] = "no-network-unix-inference-v1"
+        env["PYTHONUTF8"] = "1"
+        env["PYTHONIOENCODING"] = "utf-8"
+        env["NO_COLOR"] = "1"
+        env["CI"] = "1"
+        env["GA_PMA_UPSTREAM_ENABLED"] = "1" if args.condition == "B" else "0"
+        env["GA_PMA_ENABLED"] = env["GA_MONITOR_ENABLED"] = "0"
+        env["GA_M0_MONITOR_ENABLED"] = "0"
+        if args.condition == "B":
+            env.update(GA_PMA_UPSTREAM_REQUESTS="/run/pma-bridge/requests",
+                       GA_PMA_UPSTREAM_RESPONSES="/run/pma-bridge/responses",
+                       GA_PMA_UPSTREAM_DEADLINE_EPOCH=str(deadline))
+        (root / "archive" / "offline_command.json").write_text(json.dumps({
+            "argv": command, "public_env": {key: env[key] for key in (
+                "GA_RUN_ISOLATION", "GA_PMA_UPSTREAM_ENABLED", "GA_PMA_ENABLED",
+                "GA_MONITOR_ENABLED")}}, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
+        with (root / "archive" / "harbor.stdout.log").open("wb") as out, (
+                root / "archive" / "harbor.stderr.log").open("wb") as err:
+            harbor = subprocess.Popen(command, cwd=repo, env=env, stdout=out, stderr=err)
+            mounts = []
+            try:
+                until = time.time() + args.timeout_sec + 120
+                while harbor.poll() is None:
+                    if time.time() >= until:
+                        raise subprocess.TimeoutExpired(command, args.timeout_sec + 120)
+                    current_mounts = capture_mounts(root)
+                    if len(current_mounts) > len(mounts):
+                        mounts = current_mounts
+                        if mounts:
+                            (root / "archive" / "actual_container_mounts.json").write_text(
+                                json.dumps(mounts, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
+                    time.sleep(.5)
+                result = harbor
+            finally:
+                if harbor.poll() is None:
+                    harbor.kill()
+                    harbor.wait(timeout=10)
+        complete_mounts = capture_mounts(root, include_stopped=True)
+        if len(complete_mounts) > len(mounts):
+            mounts = complete_mounts
+            (root / "archive" / "actual_container_mounts.json").write_text(
+                json.dumps(mounts, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
+    finally:
+        if worker is not None:
+            if worker.poll() is None:
+                (root / "host_private" / "stop.request").touch()
+                try:
+                    worker.wait(timeout=10)
+                except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
+                    worker.kill()
+                    worker.wait(timeout=5)
+            worker_stdout.close()
+            worker_stderr.close()
+    worker_exit = root / "host_private" / "worker_exit.json"
+    mount_file = root / "archive" / "actual_container_mounts.json"
+    actual_mounts = json.loads(mount_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8")) if mount_file.is_file() else []
+    exposed_private = any(str(root / "host_private") in str(row.get("mounts"))
+                          for row in actual_mounts)
+    cleanup_errors = clean_owned_containers(root)
+    remaining_containers = capture_mounts(root, include_stopped=True)
+    worker_receipt = (json.loads(worker_exit.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
+                      if worker_exit.is_file() else None)
+    main = [row for row in actual_mounts if row["image_id"] == record["image_id"]]
+    spool_ok = args.condition == "A" or (len(main) == 1 and
+        {m["destination"]: m["rw"] for m in main[0]["mounts"]}.get(
+            "/run/pma-bridge/requests") is True and
+        {m["destination"]: m["rw"] for m in main[0]["mounts"]}.get(
+            "/run/pma-bridge/responses") is False)
+    integration_ok = bool(result is not None and result.returncode == 0 and
+        not exposed_private and not remaining_containers and not cleanup_errors and
+        len(main) == 1 and spool_ok and
+        (args.condition == "A" or (worker.returncode == 0 and worker_receipt is not None
+         and worker_receipt.get("task_end_saved") is True)))
+    status = {**record, "harbor_return_code": None if result is None else result.returncode,
+              "worker_return_code": None if worker is None else worker.returncode,
+              "worker_private_mounted": exposed_private if actual_mounts else "unknown",
+              "actual_container_count": len(actual_mounts),
+              "remaining_owned_containers": [row["id"] for row in remaining_containers],
+              "cleanup_errors": cleanup_errors,
+              "spool_mounts_verified": spool_ok, "integration_ok": integration_ok,
+              "worker_exit_receipt": worker_receipt}
+    (root / "archive" / "offline_result.json").write_text(
+        json.dumps(status, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
+    return status
+
+
+def main() -> None:
+    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
+    parser.add_argument("--task", choices=TASKS, required=True)
+    parser.add_argument("--condition", choices=("A", "B"), required=True)
+    parser.add_argument("--run-id", required=True)
+    parser.add_argument("--run-root", required=True)
+    parser.add_argument("--ga-source", required=True)
+    parser.add_argument("--runtime-root", required=True)
+    parser.add_argument("--task-profile-file", required=True)
+    parser.add_argument("--task-profile", default="native_claude_cc_vibe_opus48")
+    parser.add_argument("--python-home", default="cpython-3.12.12-linux-x86_64-gnu")
+    parser.add_argument("--worker-python", required=True)
+    parser.add_argument("--harbor-exe", required=True)
+    parser.add_argument("--bench-root", required=True)
+    parser.add_argument("--timeout-sec", type=int, default=180)
+    parser.add_argument("--execution-authorized", action="store_true")
+    args = parser.parse_args()
+    result = run_offline(args)
+    print(json.dumps(result, indent=2))
+    if not result["integration_ok"]:
+        raise SystemExit(1)
+
+

+    def test_independent_upstream_and_ga_event_prompts_match(self):
+        """Run original actor loop separately from the GA event scheduling."""

+                return (commands, False, "", public, None, LLMResponse(content=public))
+

+class PMAAuditIntegrityError(BaseException):
+    """Must cross the upstream `except Exception` phase-silence handler."""
+
+
+def append_private_audit(path: Path, row: dict) -> None:
+    try:
+        with path.open("a", encoding="utf-8") as stream:
+            stream.write(json.dumps(row, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True,
+                                    default=str) + "\n")
+            stream.flush()
+            os.fsync(stream.fileno())
+    except Exception as exc:
+        raise PMAAuditIntegrityError("private PMA audit write failed") from exc
+
+
+def install_transport_audit(agent, archive: Path) -> None:
+    """Observe the original two calls without changing its exception policy."""
+    original = agent._memory_llm.call
+
+    async def audited_call(*, prompt, system, tools=None):
+        phase = "bank_management" if tools else "context_decision"
+        started = time.time()
+        status = "complete"
+        append_private_audit(archive / "memory_payload.jsonl", {
+            "phase": phase, "prompt": prompt, "system": system,
+            "tools": tools, "started_epoch": started})
+        try:
+            return await original(prompt=prompt, system=system, tools=tools)
+        except BaseException as exc:
+            status = type(exc).__name__
+            raise
+        finally:
+            append_private_audit(archive / "memory_transport.jsonl", {
+                "phase": phase, "status": status,
+                "started_epoch": started, "ended_epoch": time.time()})
+
+    agent._memory_llm.call = audited_call
+
+

    },
