===== method_discovery/curator_supervisor_convergence_v0/pma_full_upstream_ga_v1_20261013/episode.py =====
"""Pilot-only event adapter around the unmodified upstream PMA object.

This module never starts Terminus2's actor loop.  Its actor backend is a
deliberately unusable BaseLLM; only the upstream memory LiteLLM may infer.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import hashlib
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

from harbor.llms.base import BaseLLM, LLMResponse
from memory_agent.memory_enabled_agent import MemoryEnabledTerminus2


class DisabledActorLLM(BaseLLM):
    calls = 0

    async def call(self, prompt: str, **kwargs: Any) -> LLMResponse:
        self.calls += 1
        raise RuntimeError("Terminus2 actor is disabled; GA owns the task")

    def get_model_context_limit(self) -> int:
        return 200_000  # Inert metadata; the actor is never queried.

    def get_model_output_limit(self) -> int | None:
        return None


def stable_initial_text(content: str | list[dict[str, Any]]) -> str:
    """Preserve GA's actual pre-reminder user envelope deterministically."""
    if isinstance(content, str):
        return content
    if isinstance(content, list):
        return json.dumps(content, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True,
                          separators=(",", ":"))
    raise TypeError("unsupported initial GA user content")


def construct_upstream(logs_dir: Path, *, memory_api_base: str | None = None,
                       memory_api_key: str | None = None) -> MemoryEnabledTerminus2:
    actor = DisabledActorLLM()
    agent = MemoryEnabledTerminus2(
        logs_dir=logs_dir, model_name="disabled-actor-not-ga-task-model",
        llm=actor, parser_name="xml", record_terminal_session=False,
        memory_model_name="openrouter/anthropic/claude-opus-4.6",
        memory_api_base=memory_api_base, memory_api_key=memory_api_key,
        memory_temperature=0.3, memory_max_thinking_tokens=None,
        memory_keep_thinking=False, memory_use_structured_tools=True,
        trigger_interval=1, trigger_on_first_step=True,
        sliding_window_size=8, reset_status_on_episode=True,
        reset_knowledge_on_episode=False, reset_procedural_on_episode=False,
        injection_method="user_turn",
    )
    if agent._llm is not actor:
        raise RuntimeError("upstream actor backend was not disabled")
    return agent


class PMAEpisode:
    """One task, one original object, one serial asyncio loop, no actor run."""

    def __init__(self, agent: MemoryEnabledTerminus2, *, run_id: str,
                 task_id: str, archive: Path):
        self.agent = agent
        self.run_id = run_id
        self.task_id = task_id
        self.archive = archive
        self.archive.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=False)
        self.started = False
        self.ended = False
        self.finalization_attempted = False
        self.last_attempt = 0
        self.last_completed_attempt = 0
        self.last_sequence = 0
        self._responses: dict[str, tuple[str, dict[str, Any]]] = {}
        self._lock = asyncio.Lock()

    def _record(self, event: dict[str, Any], result: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        line = json.dumps({"event": event, "result": result},
                          ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True)
        with (self.archive / "events.jsonl").open("a", encoding="utf-8") as stream:
            stream.write(line + "\n")
            stream.flush()
            os.fsync(stream.fileno())

    async def handle(self, event: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
        async with self._lock:
            if not isinstance(event, dict):
                raise ValueError("event must be an object")
            if (event.get("run_id"), event.get("task_id")) != (self.run_id, self.task_id):
                raise ValueError("event identity mismatch")
            event_id = event.get("event_id")
            sequence = event.get("sequence")
            if not isinstance(event_id, str) or not event_id or type(sequence) is not int:
                raise ValueError("event ID and integer sequence required")
            digest = hashlib.sha256(json.dumps(event, sort_keys=True,
                                               ensure_ascii=False).encode()).hexdigest()
            if event_id in self._responses:
                old_digest, old_result = self._responses[event_id]
                if old_digest != digest:
                    raise ValueError("event ID reused with different content")
                return old_result
            if sequence != self.last_sequence + 1:
                raise ValueError("event sequence gap or replay with unknown receipt")
            accepted_path = self.archive / "accepted.jsonl"
            with accepted_path.open("a", encoding="utf-8") as stream:
                stream.write(json.dumps({"event_id": event_id, "sequence": sequence,
                                         "sha256": digest}, sort_keys=True) + "\n")
                stream.flush()
                os.fsync(stream.fileno())
            kind = event.get("kind")
            if kind == "task_start":
                result = await self._task_start(event)
            elif kind == "before_actor_call":
                result = self._before_actor_call(event)
            elif kind == "actor_step":
                result = await self._actor_step(event)
            elif kind == "task_end":
                result = self._task_end(event)
            else:
                raise ValueError("unknown PMA event kind")
            self._record(event, result)
            if kind == "task_end" and result.get("status") == "saved":
                self.ended = True
            self.last_sequence = sequence
            self._responses[event_id] = (digest, result)
            return result

    async def _task_start(self, event: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
        if self.started or self.ended:
            raise ValueError("task_start is only valid once")
        raw = event.get("initial_user_content")
        text = stable_initial_text(raw)
        self.agent._memory_step_in_episode = 0
        self.agent._trigger.reset()
        self.agent._accumulated_observations.clear()
        self.agent._pending_injection = None
        self.agent._injection_used = False
        self.agent._task_description = text
        if self.agent._reset_status_on_episode:
            self.agent._memory.update_status("")
        if self.agent._reset_knowledge_on_episode:
            self.agent._memory.knowledge.clear()
        if self.agent._reset_procedural_on_episode:
            self.agent._memory.procedural.clear()
        self.agent._accumulate_step(step=1, observation=text)
        triggered, reason = self.agent._trigger.should_trigger(step_count=1)
        self.started = True
        if triggered:
            await self.agent._trigger_memory_agent(step_count=1)
        return {"status": "started", "triggered": triggered, "trigger_reason": reason,
                "initial_envelope_sha256": hashlib.sha256(
                    json.dumps(raw, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True).encode()).hexdigest()}

    def _before_actor_call(self, event: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
        attempt = event.get("actor_attempt")
        if not self.started or self.ended or type(attempt) is not int or attempt != self.last_attempt + 1:
            raise ValueError("non-monotonic actor attempt")
        self.agent._memory_step_in_episode = attempt
        self.last_attempt = attempt
        reminder = self.agent._get_memory_system_prompt()
        return {"status": "reminder" if reminder else "silent", "reminder": reminder,
                "actor_attempt": attempt, "delivery": "returned_not_confirmed_in_ga_request"}

    async def _actor_step(self, event: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
        attempt = event.get("actor_attempt")
        if (not self.started or self.ended or attempt != self.last_attempt
                or attempt <= self.last_completed_attempt):
            raise ValueError("actor step does not match active attempt")
        status = event.get("step_status")
        if status in {"parse_error", "provider_cancelled", "completed"}:
            self.last_completed_attempt = attempt
            return {"status": "skipped", "reason": status, "actor_attempt": attempt}
        if status != "normal":
            raise ValueError("unknown actor step status")
        commands = event.get("commands", [])
        if not isinstance(commands, list) or not all(isinstance(c, str) for c in commands):
            raise ValueError("commands must be a list of original tool descriptions")
        observation = event.get("observation")
        if not isinstance(observation, str):
            raise ValueError("complete public observation required")
        # GA has no Terminus2 plan/analysis envelope.  Its complete public
        # response is the upstream analysis input; hidden thinking is never read.
        analysis = event.get("analysis")
        if analysis is None:
            analysis = event.get("response")
        if not isinstance(analysis, str):
            raise ValueError("complete public actor response required")
        plan = event.get("plan")
        if plan is not None and not isinstance(plan, str):
            raise ValueError("plan must be text or absent")
        step = attempt + 1
        self.agent._accumulate_step(step=step, observation=observation,
                                    analysis=analysis, plan=plan, commands=commands)
        triggered, reason = self.agent._trigger.should_trigger(step_count=step)
        if triggered:
            await self.agent._trigger_memory_agent(step_count=step)
        self.last_completed_attempt = attempt
        return {"status": "observed", "triggered": triggered,
                "trigger_reason": reason, "step_count": step}

    def _task_end(self, event: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
        if not self.started or self.ended or self.finalization_attempted:
            raise ValueError("task_end out of order")
        return self._save_final_state(event.get("terminal"), event)

    def _save_final_state(self, terminal: Any, event: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """No-inference finalization; partial bank files never certify success."""
        self.finalization_attempted = True
        try:
            self.agent.save_memory(str(self.archive / "memory.json"))
            if not all((self.archive / name).is_file() for name in
                       ("memory.json", "trajectory_memory.json")):
                raise RuntimeError("upstream did not save both memory artifacts")
            for name in ("memory.json", "trajectory_memory.json"):
                with (self.archive / name).open("r", encoding="utf-8") as stream:
                    if not isinstance(json.load(stream), dict):
                        raise RuntimeError("upstream memory artifact is not an object")
        except Exception as exc:
            self._record(event, {"status": "save_failed", "error_type": type(exc).__name__,
                                 "terminal": terminal})
            raise
        return {"status": "saved", "terminal": terminal,
                "actor_attempts": self.last_attempt}

    def finalize_without_event(self, reason: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """Bounded host shutdown after ordinary Task event channel is unavailable."""
        if self.finalization_attempted or not self.started:
            return {"status": "not_saved", "reason": reason}
        result = self._save_final_state({"result": "TASK_TERMINAL_UNKNOWN"},
                                        {"kind": "host_finalize", "reason": reason})
        self.ended = True
        return {**result, "status": "saved_without_task_end", "reason": reason}


===== method_discovery/curator_supervisor_convergence_v0/pma_full_upstream_ga_v1_20261013/event_spool.py =====
"""Small single-run file transport; host bank and raw logs are never in spool.

The caller supplies pre-created mount roots.  This module does not create a
Docker container, select a host path for one, or retry an accepted event.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import tempfile
import time
import uuid

from episode import PMAEpisode


class SpoolError(RuntimeError):
    pass


def _atomic_json(path: Path, value: dict) -> None:
    descriptor, pending = tempfile.mkstemp(prefix=".pending-", dir=path.parent)
    try:
        with os.fdopen(descriptor, "w", encoding="utf-8") as stream:
            json.dump(value, stream, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True)
            stream.flush()
            os.fsync(stream.fileno())
        os.replace(pending, path)
    finally:
        if os.path.exists(pending):
            os.unlink(pending)


class EventClient:
    """GA-side request/receipt client; timeout means delivery is unknown."""

    def __init__(self, request_dir: Path, response_dir: Path, run_id: str,
                 task_id: str, *, deadline_monotonic: float):
        self.requests = Path(request_dir).resolve(strict=True)
        self.responses = Path(response_dir).resolve(strict=True)
        self.run_id = run_id
        self.task_id = task_id
        self.deadline = deadline_monotonic
        self.sequence = 0
        if not self.requests.is_dir() or not self.responses.is_dir():
            raise SpoolError("PMA event spool unavailable")

    def exchange(self, kind: str, **fields):
        if time.monotonic() >= self.deadline:
            raise SpoolError("PMA event deadline expired before send")
        self.sequence += 1
        event_id = uuid.uuid4().hex
        request = {"schema": "pma-ga-events-v1", "kind": kind,
                   "run_id": self.run_id, "task_id": self.task_id,
                   "sequence": self.sequence, "event_id": event_id, **fields}
        target = self.requests / (event_id + ".json")
        _atomic_json(target, request)
        receipt = self.responses / target.name
        while True:
            if receipt.is_file():
                try:
                    value = json.loads(receipt.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
                except (OSError, ValueError) as exc:
                    raise SpoolError("PMA receipt unreadable") from exc
                if (value.get("run_id"), value.get("task_id"), value.get("event_id")) != (
                        self.run_id, self.task_id, event_id):
                    raise SpoolError("PMA receipt identity mismatch")
                if value.get("status") != "ok":
                    raise SpoolError("PMA host event failed; see host-private event log")
                result = value.get("result")
                if not isinstance(result, dict):
                    raise SpoolError("PMA result is not an object")
                return result
            if time.monotonic() >= self.deadline:
                raise SpoolError("PMA receipt unknown; accepted event will not be replayed")
            time.sleep(0.02)


class EventDispatcher:
    """Trusted-host single-worker dispatcher with private PMA episode state."""

    def __init__(self, episode: PMAEpisode, request_dir: Path, response_dir: Path,
                 *, deadline_epoch: float | None = None):
        self.episode = episode
        self.requests = Path(request_dir).resolve(strict=True)
        self.responses = Path(response_dir).resolve(strict=True)
        self._processed = set()
        self.deadline_epoch = deadline_epoch
        if self.requests == self.responses or not self.requests.is_dir() or not self.responses.is_dir():
            raise SpoolError("invalid host event spool")
        shared_parent = self.requests.parent if self.requests.parent == self.responses.parent else None
        if (self.episode.archive.is_relative_to(self.requests)
                or self.episode.archive.is_relative_to(self.responses)
                or (shared_parent is not None
                    and self.episode.archive.is_relative_to(shared_parent))):
            raise SpoolError("private PMA archive must not be inside shared spool")

    async def serve_once(self) -> int:
        count = 0
        for path in sorted(self.requests.glob("*.json")):
            if self.deadline_epoch is not None and time.time() >= self.deadline_epoch:
                break
            if path.name in self._processed:
                continue
            if path.is_symlink() or not path.is_file():
                raise SpoolError("event request path is not a regular file")
            try:
                request = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
            except (OSError, ValueError) as exc:
                raise SpoolError("event request unreadable") from exc
            if (not isinstance(request, dict) or request.get("schema") != "pma-ga-events-v1"
                    or path.stem != request.get("event_id")
                    or (request.get("run_id"), request.get("task_id")) != (
                        self.episode.run_id, self.episode.task_id)):
                raise SpoolError("event identity mismatch")
            try:
                result = await self.episode.handle(request)
                response = {"status": "ok", "result": result}
            except Exception as exc:
                # Do not expose the bank, prompts, raw errors or credentials to GA.
                response = {"status": "error", "error_type": type(exc).__name__}
            if self.deadline_epoch is not None and time.time() >= self.deadline_epoch:
                with (self.episode.archive / "late_results.jsonl").open(
                        "a", encoding="utf-8") as stream:
                    stream.write(json.dumps({"event_id": request["event_id"],
                                             "status": "discarded_after_deadline",
                                             "remote_execution": "unknown"}) + "\n")
                self._processed.add(path.name)
                break
            response.update(run_id=self.episode.run_id, task_id=self.episode.task_id,
                            event_id=request["event_id"])
            _atomic_json(self.responses / path.name, response)
            self._processed.add(path.name)
            count += 1
        return count


===== method_discovery/curator_supervisor_convergence_v0/pma_full_upstream_ga_v1_20261013/worker_main.py =====
"""Host-side one-task PMA worker; never installed inside the GA container."""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import asyncio
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import signal
import time

from episode import PMAEpisode, construct_upstream
from event_spool import EventDispatcher


class PMAAuditIntegrityError(BaseException):
    """Must cross the upstream `except Exception` phase-silence handler."""


def append_private_audit(path: Path, row: dict) -> None:
    try:
        with path.open("a", encoding="utf-8") as stream:
            stream.write(json.dumps(row, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True,
                                    default=str) + "\n")
            stream.flush()
            os.fsync(stream.fileno())
    except Exception as exc:
        raise PMAAuditIntegrityError("private PMA audit write failed") from exc


def install_transport_audit(agent, archive: Path) -> None:
    """Observe the original two calls without changing its exception policy."""
    original = agent._memory_llm.call

    async def audited_call(*, prompt, system, tools=None):
        phase = "bank_management" if tools else "context_decision"
        started = time.time()
        status = "complete"
        append_private_audit(archive / "memory_payload.jsonl", {
            "phase": phase, "prompt": prompt, "system": system,
            "tools": tools, "started_epoch": started})
        try:
            return await original(prompt=prompt, system=system, tools=tools)
        except BaseException as exc:
            status = type(exc).__name__
            raise
        finally:
            append_private_audit(archive / "memory_transport.jsonl", {
                "phase": phase, "status": status,
                "started_epoch": started, "ended_epoch": time.time()})

    agent._memory_llm.call = audited_call


async def serve(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    private = Path(args.private_root).resolve(strict=False)
    requests = Path(args.requests).resolve(strict=True)
    responses = Path(args.responses).resolve(strict=True)
    shared_parent = requests.parent if requests.parent == responses.parent else None
    if (private.exists() or private.is_relative_to(requests)
            or private.is_relative_to(responses)
            or (shared_parent is not None and private.is_relative_to(shared_parent))):
        raise RuntimeError("PMA private root must be fresh and outside shared spool")
    if requests == responses or requests.is_symlink() or responses.is_symlink():
        raise RuntimeError("PMA spool identity invalid")
    private.mkdir(parents=True)
    agent = construct_upstream(private / "upstream_agent",
                               memory_api_base=os.environ.get("PMA_MEMORY_API_BASE"),
                               memory_api_key=os.environ.get("PMA_MEMORY_API_KEY"))
    if args.fake_memory:
        from harbor.llms.base import LLMResponse

        async def offline_memory(*, prompt, system, tools=None):
            if tools:
                return LLMResponse(content="", tool_calls=[])
            return LLMResponse(content="<context_for_action>Offline PMA reminder.</context_for_action>")

        agent._memory_llm.call = offline_memory
    episode = PMAEpisode(agent, run_id=args.run_id, task_id=args.task_id,
                         archive=private / "archive")
    install_transport_audit(agent, episode.archive)
    dispatcher = EventDispatcher(episode, requests, responses,
                                 deadline_epoch=args.deadline_epoch)
    (private / "worker_ready.json").write_text(json.dumps({
        "run_id": args.run_id, "task_id": args.task_id,
        "deadline_epoch": args.deadline_epoch, "fake_memory": args.fake_memory},
        sort_keys=True), encoding="utf-8")
    stopped = asyncio.Event()
    for name in ("SIGTERM", "SIGINT"):
        signum = getattr(signal, name, None)
        if signum is not None:
            try:
                asyncio.get_running_loop().add_signal_handler(signum, stopped.set)
            except (NotImplementedError, RuntimeError):
                pass
    reason = "task_end"
    active = None
    try:
        while not episode.ended:
            if stopped.is_set() or (private / "stop.request").exists():
                reason = "host_stop"
                break
            if time.time() >= args.deadline_epoch:
                reason = "deadline"
                break
            active = asyncio.create_task(dispatcher.serve_once())
            while not active.done():
                await asyncio.wait({active}, timeout=0.05)
                if (stopped.is_set() or (private / "stop.request").exists()
                        or time.time() >= args.deadline_epoch):
                    reason = "stopped_or_deadline_in_flight_unknown"
                    active.cancel()
                    break
            if reason != "task_end":
                break
            await active
            active = None
            await asyncio.sleep(0.05)
    except BaseException as exc:
        reason = f"worker_error:{type(exc).__name__}"
        raise
    finally:
        if not episode.ended:
            outcome = {"reason": reason, "task_end_received": False,
                       "remote_execution": "unknown" if active is not None else "not_in_flight"}
            try:
                outcome["local_save"] = episode.finalize_without_event(reason)
            except Exception as exc:
                outcome["local_save"] = {"status": "failed", "error_type": type(exc).__name__}
            (private / "worker_stopped_without_task_end.json").write_text(
                json.dumps(outcome, sort_keys=True), encoding="utf-8")
        (private / "worker_exit.json").write_text(json.dumps({
            "task_end_saved": episode.ended and reason == "task_end",
            "reason": reason, "ended_epoch": time.time()}, sort_keys=True), encoding="utf-8")


def main() -> None:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("--run-id", required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--task-id", required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--private-root", required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--requests", required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--responses", required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--deadline-epoch", type=float, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--fake-memory", action="store_true")
    asyncio.run(serve(parser.parse_args()))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()


===== method_discovery/curator_supervisor_convergence_v0/pma_full_upstream_ga_v1_20261013/offline_launcher.py =====
"""Unarmed PMA/GA Harbor fixture.  There is deliberately no live mode here."""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import hashlib
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import shutil
import subprocess
import time

from long_context_bench.scripts.isolated_run_bundle import build_bundle, digest_tree
from method_discovery.curator_supervisor_convergence_v0.real_task_effect_pilot_preflight_20261010.pilot_offline_harbor import _offline_gateway


TASKS = {
    "db-wal-recovery": "alexgshaw/db-wal-recovery:20251031",
    "fix-code-vulnerability": "alexgshaw/fix-code-vulnerability:20251031",
}
STAGE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent


def image_identity(tag: str) -> dict:
    result = subprocess.run(["docker", "image", "inspect", tag], capture_output=True,
                            text=True, timeout=30, check=True)
    row = json.loads(result.stdout)[0]
    return {"image_id": row["Id"], "repo_digests": row.get("RepoDigests") or []}


def capture_mounts(root: Path, *, include_stopped: bool = False) -> list[dict]:
    """Read actual Compose containers, never infer isolation from path names."""
    found = []
    listed = subprocess.run(["docker", "ps", "-aq" if include_stopped else "-q"], capture_output=True,
                            text=True, timeout=10, check=True)
    for container in listed.stdout.splitlines():
        inspected = subprocess.run(["docker", "inspect", container], capture_output=True,
                                   text=True, timeout=10, check=True)
        row = json.loads(inspected.stdout)[0]
        config_files = row.get("Config", {}).get("Labels", {}).get(
            "com.docker.compose.project.config_files", "")
        if str(root / "bundle" / "isolation.compose.json") not in config_files:
            continue
        found.append({"id": row["Id"], "name": row["Name"],
                      "image_id": row["Image"],
                      "network_mode": row.get("HostConfig", {}).get("NetworkMode"),
                      "mounts": [{"source": m.get("Source"), "destination": m.get("Destination"),
                                  "rw": m.get("RW"), "type": m.get("Type")}
                                 for m in row.get("Mounts", [])]})
    return found


def clean_owned_containers(root: Path) -> list[str]:
    """Remove only Compose resources whose config path is this fresh run."""
    errors = []
    for row in capture_mounts(root, include_stopped=True):
        removed = subprocess.run(["docker", "rm", "-f", row["id"]],
                                 capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=30)
        if removed.returncode:
            errors.append(f"remove_failed:{row['id']}")
            continue
        checked = subprocess.run(["docker", "inspect", row["id"]],
                                 capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=30)
        if checked.returncode == 0 or "no such object" not in checked.stderr.lower():
            errors.append(f"removal_unconfirmed:{row['id']}")
    return errors


def prepare(args) -> dict:
    if args.task not in TASKS or args.condition not in {"A", "B"}:
        raise ValueError("unsupported PMA fixture arm")
    if args.execution_authorized:
        raise ValueError("offline launcher cannot authorize model execution")
    root = Path(args.run_root).resolve(strict=False)
    root.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=False)
    (root / "control").mkdir()
    (root / "archive").mkdir()
    task = root / "public_task"
    shutil.copytree(STAGE / "assets" / "public" / args.task, task)
    (task / "tests").mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    (task / "solution").mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    # Task only receives the public task package. The fixed PMA source and
    # private memory archive never appear in a Compose or Harbor mount.
    ga_source = Path(args.ga_source).resolve(strict=True)
    ga_stage = root / "control" / "ga_stage"
    shutil.copytree(ga_source, ga_stage, ignore=shutil.ignore_patterns(
        ".git", "temp", "tests", "__pycache__", ".pytest_cache", "*.pyc",
        "*.local.json", "file_access_stats.json", "mykey.py"))
    shutil.copy2(Path(args.task_profile_file).resolve(strict=True), ga_stage / "mykey.py")
    runtime = Path(args.runtime_root).resolve(strict=True)
    source, compose = build_bundle(root / "bundle", ga_stage, runtime,
                                   args.python_home, args.task_profile, None, 15340)
    _offline_gateway(root, runtime, args.python_home)
    manifest = json.loads((STAGE / "FOUR_RUN_MANIFEST_DRAFT.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    if manifest["execution_authorized"] is not False:
        raise RuntimeError("PMA draft unexpectedly authorized")
    identity = image_identity(TASKS[args.task])
    for name in ("requests", "responses"):
        (root / "control" / "spool" / name).mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=False)
    config = json.loads(compose.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    if args.condition == "B":
        main = config["services"]["main"]
        main["volumes"] += [
            {"type": "bind", "source": (root / "control" / "spool" / "requests").as_posix(),
             "target": "/run/pma-bridge/requests", "read_only": False},
            {"type": "bind", "source": (root / "control" / "spool" / "responses").as_posix(),
             "target": "/run/pma-bridge/responses", "read_only": True},
        ]
    compose.write_text(json.dumps(config, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
    record = {"schema": "pma-ga-offline-harbor-v1", "execution_authorized": False,
              "run_id": args.run_id, "task": args.task, "condition": args.condition,
              "image": TASKS[args.task], **identity,
              "public_task_tree_sha256": digest_tree(task),
              "ga_bundle_tree_sha256": digest_tree(source),
              "source_profile_file_sha256": hashlib.sha256(
                  Path(args.task_profile_file).read_bytes()).hexdigest(),
              "worker_private_mounted": "not_yet_inspected", "network_mode": "none_requested",
              "native_evaluator_calls": 0, "fake_provider_only": True}
    (root / "archive" / "prepared.json").write_text(
        json.dumps(record, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
    return record


def run_offline(args) -> dict:
    record = prepare(args)
    root = Path(args.run_root).resolve(strict=True)
    deadline = time.time() + args.timeout_sec
    worker = None
    worker_stdout = worker_stderr = None
    result = None
    try:
        if args.condition == "B":
            worker_stdout = (root / "archive" / "worker.stdout.log").open("wb")
            worker_stderr = (root / "archive" / "worker.stderr.log").open("wb")
            env = dict(os.environ)
            env["LITELLM_LOCAL_MODEL_COST_MAP"] = "True"
            env["PYTHONPATH"] = os.pathsep.join((str(STAGE),
                str(STAGE / "vendor" / "pma_89e5c0d" / "src"),
                str(STAGE / "vendor" / "pma_89e5c0d" / "external" / "harbor")))
            command = [str(Path(args.worker_python).resolve(strict=True)),
                       str(STAGE / "worker_main.py"), "--run-id", args.run_id,
                       "--task-id", args.task, "--private-root", str(root / "host_private"),
                       "--requests", str(root / "control" / "spool" / "requests"),
                       "--responses", str(root / "control" / "spool" / "responses"),
                       "--deadline-epoch", str(deadline), "--fake-memory"]
            worker = subprocess.Popen(command, cwd=STAGE, env=env,
                                      stdout=worker_stdout, stderr=worker_stderr)
            ready = root / "host_private" / "worker_ready.json"
            ready_deadline = min(deadline, time.time() + 10)
            while not ready.is_file():
                if worker.poll() is not None or time.time() >= ready_deadline:
                    raise RuntimeError("offline PMA worker did not become ready")
                time.sleep(.05)
            worker_record = json.loads(ready.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
            if (worker_record["run_id"], worker_record["task_id"]) != (args.run_id, args.task):
                raise RuntimeError("offline PMA worker identity mismatch")
        mounts = [
            {"type": "bind", "source": str(Path(args.runtime_root).resolve(strict=True)),
             "target": "/opt/m4-runtime", "read_only": True},
            {"type": "bind", "source": str((root / "bundle" / "source").resolve(strict=True)),
             "target": "/opt/genericagent-source", "read_only": True},
        ]
        command = [str(Path(args.harbor_exe).resolve(strict=True)), "jobs", "start",
                   "--job-name", args.run_id, "--jobs-dir", str(root / "jobs"),
                   "--path", str(root / "public_task"),
                   "--agent", ("method_discovery.curator_supervisor_convergence_v0."
                               "pma_full_upstream_ga_v1_20261013.harbor_adapter:HarborGenericAgent"),
                   "--model", "claude-opus-4-8", "--mounts", json.dumps(mounts),
                   "--extra-docker-compose", str(root / "bundle" / "isolation.compose.json"),
                   "--n-concurrent", "1", "--max-retries", "0", "--yes",
                   "--disable-verification", "--agent-timeout-multiplier",
                   format(args.timeout_sec / 900, ".15g")]
        kwargs = {"llm_no": 0, "run_id": args.run_id, "task_id": args.task,
                  "expected_model": "claude-opus-4-8", "python_home": args.python_home,
                  "ga_source_sha256": record["ga_bundle_tree_sha256"],
                  "timeout_sec": args.timeout_sec, "max_turns": 2,
                  "llm_config_name": args.task_profile, "condition_id": "pma-neutral"}
        for key, value in kwargs.items():
            command += ["--ak", f"{key}={value}"]
        env = dict(os.environ)
        repo = STAGE.parents[2]
        env["PYTHONPATH"] = os.pathsep.join((str(repo), str(repo / "GenericAgent-main"),
                                            str(Path(args.bench_root).resolve(strict=True))))
        env["GA_RUN_ISOLATION"] = "no-network-unix-inference-v1"
        env["PYTHONUTF8"] = "1"
        env["PYTHONIOENCODING"] = "utf-8"
        env["NO_COLOR"] = "1"
        env["CI"] = "1"
        env["GA_PMA_UPSTREAM_ENABLED"] = "1" if args.condition == "B" else "0"
        env["GA_PMA_ENABLED"] = env["GA_MONITOR_ENABLED"] = "0"
        env["GA_M0_MONITOR_ENABLED"] = "0"
        if args.condition == "B":
            env.update(GA_PMA_UPSTREAM_REQUESTS="/run/pma-bridge/requests",
                       GA_PMA_UPSTREAM_RESPONSES="/run/pma-bridge/responses",
                       GA_PMA_UPSTREAM_DEADLINE_EPOCH=str(deadline))
        (root / "archive" / "offline_command.json").write_text(json.dumps({
            "argv": command, "public_env": {key: env[key] for key in (
                "GA_RUN_ISOLATION", "GA_PMA_UPSTREAM_ENABLED", "GA_PMA_ENABLED",
                "GA_MONITOR_ENABLED")}}, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
        with (root / "archive" / "harbor.stdout.log").open("wb") as out, (
                root / "archive" / "harbor.stderr.log").open("wb") as err:
            harbor = subprocess.Popen(command, cwd=repo, env=env, stdout=out, stderr=err)
            mounts = []
            try:
                until = time.time() + args.timeout_sec + 120
                while harbor.poll() is None:
                    if time.time() >= until:
                        raise subprocess.TimeoutExpired(command, args.timeout_sec + 120)
                    current_mounts = capture_mounts(root)
                    if len(current_mounts) > len(mounts):
                        mounts = current_mounts
                        if mounts:
                            (root / "archive" / "actual_container_mounts.json").write_text(
                                json.dumps(mounts, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
                    time.sleep(.5)
                result = harbor
            finally:
                if harbor.poll() is None:
                    harbor.kill()
                    harbor.wait(timeout=10)
        complete_mounts = capture_mounts(root, include_stopped=True)
        if len(complete_mounts) > len(mounts):
            mounts = complete_mounts
            (root / "archive" / "actual_container_mounts.json").write_text(
                json.dumps(mounts, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
    finally:
        if worker is not None:
            if worker.poll() is None:
                (root / "host_private" / "stop.request").touch()
                try:
                    worker.wait(timeout=10)
                except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
                    worker.kill()
                    worker.wait(timeout=5)
            worker_stdout.close()
            worker_stderr.close()
    worker_exit = root / "host_private" / "worker_exit.json"
    mount_file = root / "archive" / "actual_container_mounts.json"
    actual_mounts = json.loads(mount_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8")) if mount_file.is_file() else []
    exposed_private = any(str(root / "host_private") in str(row.get("mounts"))
                          for row in actual_mounts)
    cleanup_errors = clean_owned_containers(root)
    remaining_containers = capture_mounts(root, include_stopped=True)
    worker_receipt = (json.loads(worker_exit.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
                      if worker_exit.is_file() else None)
    main = [row for row in actual_mounts if row["image_id"] == record["image_id"]]
    spool_ok = args.condition == "A" or (len(main) == 1 and
        {m["destination"]: m["rw"] for m in main[0]["mounts"]}.get(
            "/run/pma-bridge/requests") is True and
        {m["destination"]: m["rw"] for m in main[0]["mounts"]}.get(
            "/run/pma-bridge/responses") is False)
    integration_ok = bool(result is not None and result.returncode == 0 and
        not exposed_private and not remaining_containers and not cleanup_errors and
        len(main) == 1 and spool_ok and
        (args.condition == "A" or (worker.returncode == 0 and worker_receipt is not None
         and worker_receipt.get("task_end_saved") is True)))
    status = {**record, "harbor_return_code": None if result is None else result.returncode,
              "worker_return_code": None if worker is None else worker.returncode,
              "worker_private_mounted": exposed_private if actual_mounts else "unknown",
              "actual_container_count": len(actual_mounts),
              "remaining_owned_containers": [row["id"] for row in remaining_containers],
              "cleanup_errors": cleanup_errors,
              "spool_mounts_verified": spool_ok, "integration_ok": integration_ok,
              "worker_exit_receipt": worker_receipt}
    (root / "archive" / "offline_result.json").write_text(
        json.dumps(status, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
    return status


def main() -> None:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("--task", choices=TASKS, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--condition", choices=("A", "B"), required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--run-id", required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--run-root", required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--ga-source", required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--runtime-root", required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--task-profile-file", required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--task-profile", default="native_claude_cc_vibe_opus48")
    parser.add_argument("--python-home", default="cpython-3.12.12-linux-x86_64-gnu")
    parser.add_argument("--worker-python", required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--harbor-exe", required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--bench-root", required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--timeout-sec", type=int, default=180)
    parser.add_argument("--execution-authorized", action="store_true")
    args = parser.parse_args()
    result = run_offline(args)
    print(json.dumps(result, indent=2))
    if not result["integration_ok"]:
        raise SystemExit(1)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()


===== method_discovery/curator_supervisor_convergence_v0/pma_full_upstream_ga_v1_20261013/audit_export_r2.py =====
"""Mechanically export only offline, secret-free PMA/GA fixture evidence."""

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import re
import subprocess


STAGE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
REPO = STAGE.parents[2]
EVIDENCE = STAGE / "offline_evidence_r2"
RUNS = {
    "db_A": Path("E:/pma-ga-offline-db-a-20261013-r2"),
    "db_B": Path("E:/pma-ga-offline-db-b-20261013-r6"),
    "code_B": Path("E:/pma-ga-offline-code-b-20261013-r4"),
    "code_A": Path("E:/pma-ga-offline-code-a-20261013"),
}


def sanitized(raw: bytes) -> bytes:
    text = raw.decode("utf-8", errors="replace")
    text = re.sub(r"(?i)(authorization|x-api-key)([\"']?\s*[:=]\s*[\"']?)[^\s\"']+",
                  r"\1\2[REDACTED]", text)
    text = re.sub(r"\bsk-[A-Za-z0-9_-]{12,}\b", "[REDACTED_KEY]", text)
    text = re.sub(r"https?://[^\s\"']+", "[REDACTED_URL]", text)
    return text.encode("utf-8")


def save_text(name: str, data: bytes) -> None:
    target = STAGE / name
    target.write_bytes(sanitized(data))


def run_tests() -> None:
    commands = [
        (["C:/Users/hsy/AppData/Local/Temp/pma-ga-py312/Scripts/python.exe",
          "-m", "unittest", "-v", "test_episode"], STAGE),
        (["py", "-3.13", "-m", "pytest", "-q",
          "tests/test_pma_upstream_ga_bridge.py",
          "tests/test_research_agent_loop.py"], REPO / "GenericAgent-main"),
    ]
    sections = []
    for command, cwd in commands:
        env = dict(os.environ)
        env["PYTHONUTF8"] = "1"
        env["LITELLM_LOCAL_MODEL_COST_MAP"] = "True"
        result = subprocess.run(command, cwd=cwd, env=env, capture_output=True,
                                timeout=120)
        sections.append("COMMAND " + json.dumps(command) + "\nCWD " + str(cwd) +
                        "\nEXIT " + str(result.returncode) + "\nSTDOUT\n" +
                        result.stdout.decode("utf-8", "replace") + "\nSTDERR\n" +
                        result.stderr.decode("utf-8", "replace"))
    save_text("TEST_RESULTS_R2.txt", "\n\n".join(sections).encode())


def export_runs() -> dict:
    EVIDENCE.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    summary = {}
    for label, root in RUNS.items():
        target = EVIDENCE / label
        target.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
        paths = [root / "archive" / name for name in (
            "offline_result.json", "actual_container_mounts.json",
            "harbor.stdout.log", "harbor.stderr.log", "worker.stdout.log",
            "worker.stderr.log")]
        paths += sorted((root / "archive" / "fake_provider_requests").glob("*"))
        if (root / "host_private" / "archive").is_dir():
            paths += [root / "host_private" / "archive" / name for name in (
                "accepted.jsonl", "events.jsonl", "memory_transport.jsonl",
                "memory_payload.jsonl",
                "memory.json", "trajectory_memory.json")]
            paths += [root / "host_private" / "worker_exit.json"]
        paths += list((root / "jobs").rglob("task_terminal.json"))
        for source in paths:
            if source.is_file():
                relative = ("fake_provider_requests/" + source.name
                            if source.parent.name == "fake_provider_requests" else source.name)
                destination = target / relative
                destination.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
                destination.write_bytes(sanitized(source.read_bytes()))
        result = json.loads((target / "offline_result.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        summary[label] = {key: result.get(key) for key in (
            "image_id", "repo_digests", "harbor_return_code", "worker_return_code",
            "actual_container_count", "worker_private_mounted", "worker_exit_receipt")}
        request = json.loads((target / "fake_provider_requests" / "0001.json").read_text(
            encoding="utf-8"))
        summary[label]["request_sha256"] = hashlib.sha256(json.dumps(
            request["body"], ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True,
            separators=(",", ":")).encode()).hexdigest()
        summary[label]["provider_capture_count"] = len(list((target /
            "fake_provider_requests").glob("*.json")))
    for task in ("db", "code"):
        a = json.loads((EVIDENCE / f"{task}_A" / "fake_provider_requests" /
                        "0001.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))["body"]
        b = json.loads((EVIDENCE / f"{task}_B" / "fake_provider_requests" /
                        "0001.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))["body"]
        summary[task + "_parity"] = {
            "system_equal": a.get("system") == b.get("system"),
            "tools_equal": a.get("tools") == b.get("tools"),
            "model_equal": a.get("model") == b.get("model"),
            "generation_equal": {key: a.get(key) for key in
                ("max_tokens", "thinking", "temperature", "stream")} ==
                {key: b.get(key) for key in
                ("max_tokens", "thinking", "temperature", "stream")},
            "public_input_prefix_equal": b["messages"][0]["content"][0]["text"].startswith(
                a["messages"][0]["content"][0]["text"]),
            "b_reminder_visible_in_first_request": "Offline PMA reminder." in json.dumps(b),
        }
    (EVIDENCE / "SUMMARY.json").write_text(json.dumps(summary, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
    return summary


def finish_packet() -> None:
    files = [path for path in sorted(EVIDENCE.rglob("*")) if path.is_file()]
    files += [STAGE / name for name in (
        "episode.py", "event_spool.py", "worker_main.py", "offline_launcher.py",
        "audit_export_r2.py", "test_episode.py", "harbor_adapter.py",
        "FOUR_RUN_MANIFEST_PREPARED.json", "REPORT_R2.md", "TEST_RESULTS_R2.txt")]
    files += [REPO / "GenericAgent-main" / name for name in (
        "agentmain.py", "agent_loop.py", "pma_upstream_bridge.py")]
    hashes = {}
    for path in files:
        relative = path.relative_to(REPO).as_posix()
        raw = path.read_bytes()
        entry = {"sha256": hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest(), "bytes": len(raw)}
        if path.suffix == ".py" or path.name in (
                "REPORT_R2.md", "FOUR_RUN_MANIFEST_PREPARED.json"):
            blob = subprocess.run(["git", "show", "HEAD:" + relative], cwd=REPO,
                                  capture_output=True, check=True).stdout
            entry["git_blob_sha256"] = hashlib.sha256(blob).hexdigest()
            entry["git_blob_bytes"] = len(blob)
        hashes[relative] = entry
    (STAGE / "FILE_HASHES_R2.json").write_text(json.dumps(hashes, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
    diff = subprocess.run(["git", "diff", "ccb841ea1bb738246493dff8802e70eb45fa7bb3",
                           "HEAD", "--", "GenericAgent-main/agentmain.py",
                           "method_discovery/curator_supervisor_convergence_v0/"
                           "pma_full_upstream_ga_v1_20261013"], cwd=REPO,
                          capture_output=True, check=True).stdout
    sections = []
    for name in ("REPORT_R2.md", "FOUR_RUN_MANIFEST_PREPARED.json",
                 "TEST_RESULTS_R2.txt", "FILE_HASHES_R2.json"):
        sections.append("===== " + name + " =====\n" +
                        (STAGE / name).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    sections.append("===== FULL DIFF FROM ccb841ea =====\n" + diff.decode("utf-8", "replace"))
    for path in files:
        sections.append("===== " + path.relative_to(REPO).as_posix() + " =====\n" +
                        path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace"))
    (STAGE / "AUDIT_PACKET_R2.txt").write_text("\n\n".join(sections), encoding="utf-8")


def main() -> None:
    run_tests()
    summary = export_runs()
    if not all(summary[f"{task}_parity"][key] for task in ("db", "code") for key in (
            "system_equal", "tools_equal", "model_equal", "generation_equal",
            "public_input_prefix_equal", "b_reminder_visible_in_first_request")):
        raise RuntimeError("A/B offline first request parity failed")
    finish_packet()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()


===== method_discovery/curator_supervisor_convergence_v0/pma_full_upstream_ga_v1_20261013/test_episode.py =====
"""Offline tests: real upstream object and MemoryAgent, scripted LiteLLM I/O."""

import asyncio
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
from tempfile import TemporaryDirectory
import time
import unittest
from types import SimpleNamespace
import sys
from unittest.mock import patch

# Offline tests must not let LiteLLM fetch its optional price metadata.
os.environ["LITELLM_LOCAL_MODEL_COST_MAP"] = "True"
sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3] / "GenericAgent-main"))

from harbor.llms.base import LLMResponse
from memory_agent.utils.visualize import visualize_memory, visualize_memory_trajectory

from episode import DisabledActorLLM, PMAEpisode, construct_upstream
from event_spool import EventClient, EventDispatcher
from pma_upstream_bridge import UpstreamPMAClient
import worker_main


class EpisodeTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_original_lifecycle_and_duplicate_suppression(self):
        with TemporaryDirectory() as directory:
            root = Path(directory)
            agent = construct_upstream(root / "agent")
            self.assertEqual(agent._memory_llm._model_name,
                             "openrouter/anthropic/claude-opus-4.6")
            self.assertEqual(agent._memory_llm._temperature, 0.3)
            self.assertTrue(agent._memory_agent.use_structured_tools)
            calls = []

            async def fake_memory_call(*, prompt, system, tools=None):
                calls.append({"prompt": prompt, "system": system,
                              "tools": bool(tools)})
                if tools:
                    return LLMResponse(content="", tool_calls=[])
                if len(calls) == 2:
                    return LLMResponse(content="<context_for_action>Remember X</context_for_action>")
                return LLMResponse(content="<no_intervention/>")

            agent._memory_llm.call = fake_memory_call
            episode = PMAEpisode(agent, run_id="r", task_id="t", archive=root / "private")

            def event(kind, sequence, **fields):
                return {"kind": kind, "sequence": sequence,
                        "event_id": f"e{sequence}", "run_id": "r", "task_id": "t", **fields}

            async def run():
                start = event("task_start", 1, initial_user_content="Original public GA input")
                self.assertTrue((await episode.handle(start))["triggered"])
                self.assertEqual(await episode.handle(start), await episode.handle(start))
                before = await episode.handle(event("before_actor_call", 2, actor_attempt=1))
                self.assertIn("Remember X", before["reminder"])
                self.assertEqual(before["delivery"], "returned_not_confirmed_in_ga_request")
                observed = await episode.handle(event(
                    "actor_step", 3, actor_attempt=1, step_status="normal",
                    response="PUBLIC_DIAGNOSTIC: inspect both files",
                    commands=["tool A", "tool B"], observation="two public results"))
                self.assertEqual(observed["step_count"], 2)
                self.assertEqual((await episode.handle(event(
                    "before_actor_call", 4, actor_attempt=2)))["status"], "silent")
                skipped = await episode.handle(event(
                    "actor_step", 5, actor_attempt=2, step_status="parse_error"))
                self.assertEqual(skipped["status"], "skipped")
                self.assertEqual((await episode.handle(event(
                    "before_actor_call", 6, actor_attempt=3)))["actor_attempt"], 3)
                await episode.handle(event("actor_step", 7, actor_attempt=3,
                                           step_status="completed"))
                self.assertEqual((await episode.handle(event(
                    "task_end", 8, terminal="CURRENT_TASK_DONE")))["status"], "saved")

            asyncio.run(run())
            self.assertEqual(len(calls), 4)  # Two real PMA phases at each trigger.
            self.assertEqual([x["tools"] for x in calls], [True, False, True, False])
            self.assertEqual(agent._accumulated_observations[1]["commands"], ["tool A", "tool B"])
            self.assertEqual(agent._accumulated_observations[1]["step"], 2)
            self.assertIn("two public results", calls[2]["prompt"])
            for call in calls[2:4]:
                self.assertIn("PUBLIC_DIAGNOSTIC: inspect both files", call["prompt"])
                self.assertIn("tool A", call["prompt"])
                self.assertIn("tool B", call["prompt"])
                self.assertIn("two public results", call["prompt"])
            self.assertEqual(agent._llm.calls, 0)
            self.assertIsInstance(agent._llm, DisabledActorLLM)
            self.assertTrue((root / "private" / "memory.json").is_file())
            self.assertTrue((root / "private" / "trajectory_memory.json").is_file())
            self.assertIsNotNone(visualize_memory(root / "private" / "memory.json"))
            self.assertIsNotNone(visualize_memory_trajectory(
                root / "private" / "trajectory_memory.json"))

    def test_actor_never_usable(self):
        with TemporaryDirectory() as directory:
            agent = construct_upstream(Path(directory))
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(RuntimeError, "actor is disabled"):
                asyncio.run(agent._llm.call("must fail"))

    def test_second_save_failure_never_marks_clean_end(self):
        with TemporaryDirectory() as directory:
            root = Path(directory)
            agent = construct_upstream(root / "agent")
            agent._trigger.should_trigger = lambda step_count: (False, "fixture")
            episode = PMAEpisode(agent, run_id="r", task_id="t", archive=root / "private")
            original_save = agent.save_memory

            def fail_after_bank(path):
                agent._memory.save_to_file(Path(path))
                raise OSError("trajectory write failed")

            agent.save_memory = fail_after_bank

            async def run():
                await episode.handle({"kind": "task_start", "sequence": 1,
                                      "event_id": "a", "run_id": "r", "task_id": "t",
                                      "initial_user_content": "public"})
                with self.assertRaises(OSError):
                    await episode.handle({"kind": "task_end", "sequence": 2,
                                          "event_id": "b", "run_id": "r", "task_id": "t",
                                          "terminal": {"result": "CURRENT_TASK_DONE"}})

            asyncio.run(run())
            self.assertFalse(episode.ended)
            self.assertTrue(episode.finalization_attempted)
            self.assertFalse((root / "private" / "trajectory_memory.json").exists())
            self.assertIn("save_failed", (root / "private" / "events.jsonl").read_text())
            agent.save_memory = original_save

    def test_worker_deadline_cancels_inflight_without_late_receipt(self):
        with TemporaryDirectory() as directory:
            root = Path(directory)
            requests, responses = root / "shared" / "requests", root / "shared" / "responses"
            requests.mkdir(parents=True)
            responses.mkdir()
            request = {"schema": "pma-ga-events-v1", "kind": "task_start",
                       "sequence": 1, "event_id": "first", "run_id": "r",
                       "task_id": "t", "initial_user_content": "public"}
            (requests / "first.json").write_text(json.dumps(request), encoding="utf-8")
            agent = construct_upstream(root / "agent")

            async def slow_memory(*, prompt, system, tools=None):
                await asyncio.sleep(5)
                return LLMResponse(content="<no_intervention/>", tool_calls=[])

            agent._memory_llm.call = slow_memory
            args = SimpleNamespace(private_root=str(root / "private"),
                                   requests=str(requests), responses=str(responses),
                                   run_id="r", task_id="t", fake_memory=False,
                                   deadline_epoch=time.time() + .25)
            with patch.object(worker_main, "construct_upstream", return_value=agent):
                asyncio.run(worker_main.serve(args))
            self.assertFalse((responses / "first.json").exists())
            exit_record = json.loads((root / "private" / "worker_exit.json").read_text())
            self.assertFalse(exit_record["task_end_saved"])
            self.assertEqual(exit_record["reason"], "stopped_or_deadline_in_flight_unknown")
            self.assertTrue((root / "private" / "archive" / "memory.json").is_file())

    def test_original_phase_errors_are_audited_without_changing_silence(self):
        for failed_phase in ("bank_management", "context_decision"):
            with self.subTest(failed_phase=failed_phase), TemporaryDirectory() as directory:
                root = Path(directory)
                agent = construct_upstream(root / "agent")

                async def fake_call(*, prompt, system, tools=None):
                    phase = "bank_management" if tools else "context_decision"
                    if phase == failed_phase:
                        raise RuntimeError("scripted upstream failure")
                    if tools:
                        return LLMResponse(content="", tool_calls=[])
                    return LLMResponse(content="<no_intervention/>")

                agent._memory_llm.call = fake_call
                episode = PMAEpisode(agent, run_id="r", task_id="t", archive=root / "private")
                worker_main.install_transport_audit(agent, episode.archive)

                async def run():
                    await episode.handle({"kind": "task_start", "sequence": 1,
                                          "event_id": "start", "run_id": "r", "task_id": "t",
                                          "initial_user_content": "public"})
                    return await episode.handle({"kind": "before_actor_call",
                                                 "sequence": 2, "event_id": "before",
                                                 "run_id": "r", "task_id": "t",
                                                 "actor_attempt": 1})

                self.assertEqual(asyncio.run(run())["status"], "silent")
                rows = [json.loads(line) for line in (episode.archive /
                        "memory_transport.jsonl").read_text().splitlines()]
                self.assertEqual([row["phase"] for row in rows],
                                 ["bank_management", "context_decision"])
                self.assertEqual(rows[0 if failed_phase == "bank_management" else 1]
                                 ["status"], "RuntimeError")

    def test_original_and_ga_initial_phase_failure_matrix(self):
        """Original loop is the reference; no adapter method drives it."""
        for failed_phase in (None, "bank_management", "context_decision"):
            with self.subTest(failed_phase=failed_phase), TemporaryDirectory() as directory:
                root = Path(directory)
                captured = {}
                agents = {}
                for arm in ("reference", "ga"):
                    agent = construct_upstream(root / arm)
                    agents[arm] = agent
                    captured[arm] = []

                    async def scripted(*, prompt, system, tools=None, arm=arm):
                        phase = "bank_management" if tools else "context_decision"
                        captured[arm].append((phase, prompt, system))
                        if phase == failed_phase:
                            raise RuntimeError("same scripted phase failure")
                        if tools:
                            return LLMResponse(content="", tool_calls=[])
                        return LLMResponse(content="<no_intervention/>")

                    agent._memory_llm.call = scripted
                reference = agents["reference"]
                reference._context = SimpleNamespace()
                reference._max_episodes = 0
                reference._enable_summarize = False
                reference._session = SimpleNamespace(is_session_alive=lambda: asyncio.sleep(0, result=True))
                class UnusedFakeChat:
                    async def chat(self, *args, **kwargs):
                        raise AssertionError("zero-episode reference must not call actor")

                asyncio.run(reference._run_agent_loop("Identical initial Task", UnusedFakeChat()))
                episode = PMAEpisode(agents["ga"], run_id="r", task_id="t",
                                     archive=root / "private")
                asyncio.run(episode.handle({"kind": "task_start", "sequence": 1,
                                            "event_id": "start", "run_id": "r",
                                            "task_id": "t",
                                            "initial_user_content": "Identical initial Task"}))
                self.assertEqual(captured["reference"], captured["ga"])
                self.assertEqual(reference._memory.status, agents["ga"]._memory.status)
                self.assertEqual(reference._pending_injection, agents["ga"]._pending_injection)
                self.assertEqual(len(captured["ga"]), 2)

    def test_private_phase_audit_failure_is_not_upstream_silence(self):
        with TemporaryDirectory() as directory:
            root = Path(directory)
            requests, responses = root / "shared" / "requests", root / "shared" / "responses"
            requests.mkdir(parents=True)
            responses.mkdir()
            agent = construct_upstream(root / "agent")
            async def fake_call(*, prompt, system, tools=None):
                return LLMResponse(content="<no_intervention/>", tool_calls=[])
            agent._memory_llm.call = fake_call
            episode = PMAEpisode(agent, run_id="r", task_id="t", archive=root / "private")
            worker_main.install_transport_audit(agent, episode.archive)
            (requests / "first.json").write_text(json.dumps({
                "schema": "pma-ga-events-v1", "kind": "task_start",
                "event_id": "first", "sequence": 1, "run_id": "r", "task_id": "t",
                "initial_user_content": "public"}), encoding="utf-8")
            dispatcher = EventDispatcher(episode, requests, responses)
            with patch.object(worker_main, "append_private_audit",
                              side_effect=worker_main.PMAAuditIntegrityError("denied")):
                with self.assertRaises(worker_main.PMAAuditIntegrityError):
                    asyncio.run(dispatcher.serve_once())
            self.assertFalse((responses / "first.json").exists())

    def test_file_event_bridge_keeps_private_bank_outside_shared_spool(self):
        with TemporaryDirectory() as directory:
            root = Path(directory)
            requests, responses = root / "shared" / "requests", root / "shared" / "responses"
            requests.mkdir(parents=True)
            responses.mkdir()
            agent = construct_upstream(root / "agent")
            calls = []

            async def fake_memory_call(*, prompt, system, tools=None):
                calls.append(bool(tools))
                return LLMResponse(content="<no_intervention/>", tool_calls=[])

            agent._memory_llm.call = fake_memory_call
            episode = PMAEpisode(agent, run_id="r", task_id="t", archive=root / "host_private")
            dispatcher = EventDispatcher(episode, requests, responses)
            client = EventClient(requests, responses, "r", "t",
                                 deadline_monotonic=time.monotonic() + 5)

            async def run():
                pending = asyncio.create_task(asyncio.to_thread(
                    client.exchange, "task_start", initial_user_content="GA public input"))
                while not pending.done():
                    await dispatcher.serve_once()
                    await asyncio.sleep(.01)
                self.assertEqual((await pending)["status"], "started")
                self.assertEqual(await dispatcher.serve_once(), 0)

            asyncio.run(run())
            self.assertEqual(calls, [True, False])
            self.assertFalse((root / "shared" / "host_private").exists())
            self.assertFalse(any("memory" in p.name for p in requests.iterdir()))
            receipt = json.loads(next(responses.iterdir()).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
            self.assertEqual(receipt["status"], "ok")
            self.assertNotIn("memory_stats", receipt["result"])

    def test_actual_ga_client_event_matches_host_dispatcher(self):
        with TemporaryDirectory() as directory:
            root = Path(directory)
            requests, responses = root / "shared" / "requests", root / "shared" / "responses"
            requests.mkdir(parents=True)
            responses.mkdir()
            agent = construct_upstream(root / "agent")

            async def fake_memory_call(*, prompt, system, tools=None):
                return LLMResponse(content="<no_intervention/>", tool_calls=[])

            agent._memory_llm.call = fake_memory_call
            episode = PMAEpisode(agent, run_id="r", task_id="t", archive=root / "private")
            dispatcher = EventDispatcher(episode, requests, responses)
            ga_client = UpstreamPMAClient(run_id="r", task_id="t", requests=requests,
                                          responses=responses, deadline=time.time() + 5)

            async def run():
                pending = asyncio.create_task(asyncio.to_thread(
                    ga_client.exchange, 'task_start', initial_user_content='GA input'))
                while not pending.done():
                    await dispatcher.serve_once()
                    await asyncio.sleep(.01)
                self.assertEqual((await pending)['status'], 'started')
                self.assertEqual(episode.last_sequence, 1)

            asyncio.run(run())

    def test_unmodified_upstream_actor_loop_reference_first_two_steps(self):
        """Reference runs upstream _run_agent_loop, not the GA event adapter."""
        with TemporaryDirectory() as directory:
            root = Path(directory)
            agent = construct_upstream(root / "reference")
            memory_requests = []

            async def fake_memory_call(*, prompt, system, tools=None):
                memory_requests.append((bool(tools), prompt))
                if tools:
                    return LLMResponse(content="", tool_calls=[])
                if len(memory_requests) == 2:
                    return LLMResponse(content="<context_for_action>Remember X</context_for_action>")
                return LLMResponse(content="<no_intervention/>")

            agent._memory_llm.call = fake_memory_call
            agent._context = SimpleNamespace()
            agent._max_episodes = 2
            agent._enable_summarize = False
            agent._session = SimpleNamespace(is_session_alive=lambda: asyncio.sleep(0, result=True))
            agent._setup_episode_logging = lambda *a: None
            agent._record_asciinema_marker = lambda *a: None
            agent._record_step_full = lambda *a, **k: None
            agent._record_step = lambda *a, **k: None
            agent._dump_trajectory = lambda: None
            seen_actor_prompts = []

            class FakeChat:
                total_input_tokens = 0
                total_output_tokens = 0
                total_cache_tokens = 0
                total_cost = 0

                async def chat(self, prompt, logging_path=None, **kwargs):
                    seen_actor_prompts.append(prompt)
                    return LLMResponse(content="actor")

            actor_calls = 0

            async def fake_actor_interaction(chat, prompt, *args):
                nonlocal actor_calls
                await chat.chat(prompt)
                actor_calls += 1
                if actor_calls == 1:
                    return (["tool A", "tool B"], False, "", None, None,
                            LLMResponse(content="actor"))
                return ([], False, "ERROR: parse", None, None,
                        LLMResponse(content="bad actor format"))

            agent._handle_llm_interaction = fake_actor_interaction
            agent._execute_commands = lambda commands, session: asyncio.sleep(
                0, result=(False, "two public results"))
            asyncio.run(agent._run_agent_loop("Original public GA input", FakeChat()))
            self.assertEqual(actor_calls, 2)
            self.assertIn("Remember X", seen_actor_prompts[0])
            self.assertEqual(seen_actor_prompts[1], "two public results")
            self.assertEqual([entry["step"] for entry in agent._accumulated_observations],
                             [1, 2])
            self.assertEqual(agent._accumulated_observations[1]["commands"],
                             ["tool A", "tool B"])
            self.assertEqual(len(memory_requests), 4)
            self.assertEqual(agent._llm.calls, 0)

    def test_independent_upstream_and_ga_event_prompts_match(self):
        """Run original actor loop separately from the GA event scheduling."""
        with TemporaryDirectory() as directory:
            root = Path(directory)
            public = "PUBLIC_ANALYSIS: both files affect the transaction"
            commands = ["tool alpha --read", "tool beta --read"]
            observation = json.dumps({"content": "RESULT_ALPHA and RESULT_BETA",
                                      "tool_results": ["RESULT_ALPHA", "RESULT_BETA"]},
                                     ensure_ascii=False)
            requests = {}
            agents = {}
            for arm in ("original", "ga"):
                agent = construct_upstream(root / arm)
                agents[arm] = agent
                requests[arm] = []

                async def memory_call(*, prompt, system, tools=None, arm=arm):
                    requests[arm].append((prompt, system,
                        json.dumps(tools, sort_keys=True, default=str) if tools is not None else None))
                    if tools:
                        return LLMResponse(content="", tool_calls=[])
                    return LLMResponse(content="<context_for_action>Keep the public clue.</context_for_action>")

                agent._memory_llm.call = memory_call

            reference = agents["original"]
            reference._context = SimpleNamespace()
            reference._max_episodes = 2
            reference._enable_summarize = False
            reference._session = SimpleNamespace(is_session_alive=lambda: asyncio.sleep(0, result=True))
            reference._setup_episode_logging = lambda *a: None
            reference._record_asciinema_marker = lambda *a: None
            reference._record_step_full = lambda *a, **k: None
            reference._dump_trajectory = lambda: None

            class FakeChat:
                total_input_tokens = total_output_tokens = total_cache_tokens = total_cost = 0

                async def chat(self, prompt, **kwargs):
                    return LLMResponse(content=public)

            actor_attempts = 0

            async def reference_actor(chat, prompt, *args):
                nonlocal actor_attempts
                await chat.chat(prompt)
                actor_attempts += 1
                if actor_attempts == 2:
                    return ([], False, "ERROR: bad actor envelope", None, None,
                            LLMResponse(content="bad actor envelope"))
                return (commands, False, "", public, None, LLMResponse(content=public))

            reference._handle_llm_interaction = reference_actor
            reference._execute_commands = lambda cmds, session: asyncio.sleep(
                0, result=(False, observation))
            asyncio.run(reference._run_agent_loop("Same public GA task", FakeChat()))

            ga = agents["ga"]
            episode = PMAEpisode(ga, run_id="r", task_id="t", archive=root / "private")

            async def ga_events():
                def event(kind, sequence, **fields):
                    return {"kind": kind, "event_id": f"e{sequence}", "sequence": sequence,
                            "run_id": "r", "task_id": "t", **fields}

                await episode.handle(event("task_start", 1,
                                           initial_user_content="Same public GA task"))
                first = await episode.handle(event("before_actor_call", 2, actor_attempt=1))
                self.assertIn("Keep the public clue.", first["reminder"])
                await episode.handle(event("actor_step", 3, actor_attempt=1,
                                           step_status="normal", response=public,
                                           commands=commands, observation=observation))
                await episode.handle(event("before_actor_call", 4, actor_attempt=2))
                await episode.handle(event("actor_step", 5, actor_attempt=2,
                                           step_status="parse_error",
                                           response="bad actor envelope"))

            asyncio.run(ga_events())
            self.assertEqual(requests["original"], requests["ga"])
            self.assertEqual(reference._accumulated_observations,
                             ga._accumulated_observations)
            self.assertEqual(reference._pending_injection, ga._pending_injection)
            self.assertEqual(len(requests["ga"]), 4)
            self.assertIsNotNone(requests["ga"][0][2])
            self.assertEqual(actor_attempts, 2)
            self.assertIn(public, requests["ga"][2][0])
            self.assertIn("RESULT_BETA", requests["ga"][3][0])


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()


===== method_discovery/curator_supervisor_convergence_v0/pma_full_upstream_ga_v1_20261013/harbor_adapter.py =====
"""Pilot-only forwarding overlay for a byte-pinned existing Harbor GA adapter.

Loaded only by an explicitly selected research --agent path.  The ordinary
adapter, A condition and other experiments do not import this module.
"""

import hashlib
from pathlib import Path

from adapters import harbor_ga_agent as original


EXPECTED_ADAPTER_SHA256 = "8a4513c6b28befe747d581d22e94cd2715f20234c63115e0ea424a56d033c3a0"
FORWARD = (
    "GA_PMA_UPSTREAM_ENABLED",
    "GA_PMA_UPSTREAM_REQUESTS",
    "GA_PMA_UPSTREAM_RESPONSES",
    "GA_PMA_UPSTREAM_DEADLINE_EPOCH",
)

actual = hashlib.sha256(Path(original.__file__).read_bytes()).hexdigest()
if actual != EXPECTED_ADAPTER_SHA256:
    raise RuntimeError("PMA Harbor base adapter byte identity mismatch")
if any(name in original.FORWARDED_ENV_VARS for name in FORWARD):
    raise RuntimeError("PMA Harbor forwarding unexpectedly already present")
original.FORWARDED_ENV_VARS = original.FORWARDED_ENV_VARS + FORWARD


class HarborGenericAgent(original.HarborGenericAgent):
    """Same original run/setup implementation, with only four extra env names."""

    @staticmethod
    def name() -> str:
        return "generic-agent-harbor-pma-upstream"
